TL;DR: LimiX-2 是 LimiX 系列的新模型，通过模型与数据扩展并遵循既定缩放定律开发。它采用上下文机制网络（CMN）范式，用上下文条件掩码建模（CCMM）进行预训练，将上下文学习目标从目标中心预测转向机制导向的联合建模，在多个表格基准上超越现有模型，并具备因果感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统表格 PFN 以 p(y|x, D_context) 为目标中心预测的局限，将上下文学习的组织原则转变为学习数据生成的联合结构 p(x, y|D_context)，从而更全面地捕捉数据生成机制，并提升模型的因果感知能力。

Method: LimiX-2 采用上下文机制网络（CMN）范式，围绕学习 p(x, y|D_context) 的上下文相关联合结构表示进行设计；预训练使用上下文条件掩码建模（CCMM），训练数据为结构性因果模型（SCM）生成的合成数据集，覆盖多样的图结构、函数机制与观测过程，并遵循既定的缩放定律进行模型与数据扩展。

Result: 在 TabArena、TALENT 和 BCCO 三个基准上的评估显示，LimiX-2 超越了当前的数据集特定模型与表格基础模型。此外，CMN 范式赋予模型因果感知能力，其特征注意力编码了直接因果关系，能够实现准确的因果骨架恢复。

Conclusion: 通过机制导向的联合建模与 SCM 合成数据预训练，LimiX-2 在表格数据预测性能上取得领先，同时实现了因果骨架恢复，证明了 CMN 范式在提升预测能力与因果理解方面的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce LimiX-2, a new model in the LimiX family, developed through model and data scaling guided by our previously established scaling laws. LimiX-2 adopts the Contextual Mechanism Networks (CMNs) paradigm and is pretrained with Context-Conditional Masked Modeling (CCMM). CMNs shifts the organizing principle of in-context learning from target-centric prediction to mechanism-oriented joint modeling. Rather than centering the network on the $p(y \mid x, D_{\mathrm{context}})$ objective of conventional tabular PFNs, it is designed around learning $p(x, y \mid D_{\mathrm{context}})$, a context-dependent representation of the joint structure underlying data generation. Pretraining uses synthetic datasets generated by structural causal models (SCMs) spanning diverse graph structures, functional mechanisms, and observation processes. Evaluations on TabArena, TALENT, and BCCO show that LimiX-2 outperforms current dataset-specific models and tabular foundation models. Beyond predictive performance, the CMN paradigm also promotes causal awareness in LimiX-2: its feature attention encodes direct causal relationships, enabling accurate causal skeleton recovery.

</details>


### [239] [Verifiable Social Reasoning for LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17496)
*Amir Taubenfeld,Zorik Gekhman,Avigail Grinstein-Dabush,Itay Laish,Ariel Goldstein,Marian Croak,Avinatan Hassidim,Yossi Matias,Amir Feder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fuse 是一个多智能体仿真框架，通过在受控社交情境中生成可验证的真实标签，来评测 LLM 助手在用户转述（user-mediated）场景下的社交推理能力，并据此对 12 个 LLM 进行了系统分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以评测 LLM 在咨询式社交推理中的表现，原因有二：一是该场景要求助手从用户主观叙事中了解社交情境；二是社交属性（如他人意图）缺乏可验证的真实标签。因此需要一种能兼具真实咨询设定与可验证 grounding 的评测方案。

Method: 提出 Fuse 多智能体仿真框架：一个怀有隐藏动机的目标智能体与其他智能体（含代表用户的智能体）互动，用户随后向被评测的助手咨询以推断目标动机，从而在构造上提供可验证的真实标签。仿真忠实度通过含 24k 标注的人类研究验证。该方法被应用于 12 个 LLM，并系统隔离多个关键因素进行分析。

Result: 实验显示：(i) 用户转述加剧了社交推理的固有难度；(ii) LLM 对偏见的用户表述表现出系统性敏感性；(iii) 模型所需的细节量可能超过人类做出正确预测所需；(iv) 对话更长并不总能提升性能，尽管提供了澄清提问的机会。并开源了 Fuse 及包含 21k 样本的数据集。

Conclusion: Fuse 为研究用户转述式社交推理提供了可靠且可验证的评测框架，揭示了 LLM 在这一复杂场景下的多项系统性不足，为未来改进 LLM 社交智能提供了分析工具与基准。

Abstract: LLM assistants are widely used for daily social advice, yet evaluating their social reasoning in such consultation settings remains challenging since (i) it requires setups where the assistant learns about social situations from subjective user narratives, and (ii) social properties, such as others' intentions, typically lack verifiable ground truth. To address these challenges, we introduce Fuse, a multi-agent simulation framework for studying user-mediated social reasoning. In Fuse, a target agent with a hidden motive interacts with other agents including one representing the user, who then consults the evaluated assistant to infer the target's motive, providing verifiable ground truth by construction. Simulation faithfulness is validated through a human study with 24k annotations. We apply Fuse to 12 LLMs and demonstrate its analytical utility by systematically isolating key factors, showing that (i) user mediation compounds the inherent difficulty of social reasoning; (ii) LLMs exhibit systematic sensitivity to biased user framing; (iii) models can require more details than humans need to reach a correct prediction; and (iv) longer conversations do not always improve performance despite providing opportunities for clarifying questions. We open-source Fuse and a dataset with 21k examples.

</details>


### [240] [ScienceBuddy: Recursive-in-Recursive Self-Improvement for Interactive Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17523)
*Shuhan Xue,Jianyuan Zhong,Ziyuan Nan,Wenbin Li,Zhaochen Yu,Jinchao Ding,Qiang Gao,Pengyu Zhan,Yuntong Zhang,Tian Cheng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍并发布 ScienceBuddy，一个交互式科研工作空间，通过“递归套递归的自改进”范式（内层改进 harness、外层强化学习训练模型）使科学智能体在不断协作中持续进化，并公开案例研究与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体往往与研究者日常工作流程脱节，难以持续利用研究者的请求、反馈和执行证据进行自我改进。作者希望将不断改进的科学智能体无缝融入科研日常，推动“发现智能”（discovery intelligence）——即科学 AI 能通过与研究者持续协作自我进化，随所支持的研究一同发展。

Method: 提出并实现 ScienceBuddy 交互式科研工作空间，将研究者的请求、反馈和执行证据转化为任务与评估准则（rubrics），用于持续学习。核心是“递归套递归的自改进”（recursive-in-recursive self-improvement）范式：内层递归在模型固定时改进 harness（实验/评估框架），外层递归在改进后的 harness 下训练模型（强化学习）。harness 演化塑造训练经验，模型学习又为 harness 调整创造新机会，二者相互促进形成正反馈闭环。文中展示了研究者交互、harness 精化与模型学习的案例，基准覆盖四个科学任务族。

Result: 通过案例研究展示了研究者的交互、harness 的逐步精化以及模型能力的提升；基准案例覆盖四个科学任务族，说明该范式能持续将人类反馈和执行证据转化为改进信号，驱动 harness 与模型协同进化。

Conclusion: ScienceBuddy 作为研究产品发布，使这一自改进范式可被科学界使用，是迈向“发现智能”的一步：科学 AI 通过与研究者持续协作不断进化，并与其所支持的研究共同发展。项目网址：http://science-buddy.io

Abstract: We introduce and release ScienceBuddy, an interactive scientific research workspace that brings continually improving scientific agents into researchers' everyday workflows. ScienceBuddy supports researchers in carrying out scientific tasks while transforming their requests, feedback, and execution evidence into tasks and evaluation rubrics for continual learning. At its core is recursive-in-recursive self-improvement, a paradigm that couples harness evolution with model reinforcement learning: the inner recursion improves the harness with the model fixed, while the outer recursion trains the model under the improved harness. Harness evolution shapes training experience, and model learning creates new opportunities for harness adaptation. We present case studies of researcher interaction, harness refinement, and model learning, with the benchmark cases spanning four scientific task families. By releasing ScienceBuddy as a research product, we make this paradigm available to the scientific community and take a step toward discovery intelligence: scientific AI that advances through sustained collaboration with researchers and evolves alongside the research it supports. Website: http://science-buddy.io

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Component-weighted sparse group LASSO for finite mixture regression models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16050)
*Vinay Joshy,Zeny Feng,Grace Stelter,Lorna E Deeth,Alysha Cooper*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文提出一种组件加权的稀疏组LASSO正则化方法，在有限混合回归模型中同时实现组级（跨子总体剔除完全无关协变量）与个体级（子总体内剔除无关协变量）的双层变量选择，并利用Majorization-Minimization算法进行优化求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质数据常含潜在子总体，有限混合回归（FMR）模型因允许回归系数在组件间变化而有效。变量选择需在两个层面进行：组级（删除所有子总体中完全无关的协变量）和个体级（删除各子总体内部无关的协变量）。但现有FMR变量选择方法仅关注个体级系数选择，缺乏组级变量消除机制。

Method: 提出组件加权的稀疏组LASSO正则化方法，适用于指数族有限混合回归模型，可同时在组级与个体级进行变量选择；并基于Majorization-Minimization（MM）算法开发了新的优化求解流程。

Result: 在高斯与泊松混合回归设置下的模拟研究表明，该方法能在组级和个体级两个层面可靠地恢复真实的稀疏结构；并对两个真实数据集进行了分析验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法填补了FMR中组级变量选择机制的空白，实现了双层同时稀疏选择，模拟与实证结果均验证其有效性与实用性。

Abstract: When analyzing heterogeneous data with latent subpopulations, finite mixture regression (FMR) models are effective as they allow for variations in regression coefficients across mixture components. Variable selection is important at two levels: (1) at the group level by removing completely irrelevant covariates across all subpopulations and (2) at the individual level by removing irrelevant covariates within each subpopulation. However, existing variable selection methods for FMR focus solely on individual-level regression coefficient selection, providing no mechanism for group-level variable elimination. We introduce a component-weighted sparse group LASSO regularization method that performs efficient variable selection at both levels simultaneously in finite mixture regression models belonging to the exponential family. We develop a novel optimization procedure through a Majorization-Minimization algorithm. Simulation studies under Gaussian and Poisson mixture regression settings demonstrate reliable recovery of the true sparsity structure at both levels. The method is illustrated by analyzing two real datasets.

</details>


### [242] [Coherent and robust Bayesian inference under informative sampling via sample-level weight models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16225)
*Kosuke Morikawa,Jae Kwang Kim,Won Chang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补的贝叶斯方法，用于在信息性抽样（informative sampling）下进行解析推断：第一种通过建模权重分布构建相干似然；第二种利用 Neyman 正交化的设计加权得分并结合损失似然自助法（loss-likelihood bootstrap），在权重模型误设下仍保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息性抽样中，抽样机制与结果变量相关，直接使用标准贝叶斯推断会导致偏差。现有方法要么依赖正确指定的抽样/权重模型而缺乏稳健性，要么能稳健估计但缺乏严格的似然基础。本文旨在既保留贝叶斯相干似然的解释力，又提供对权重模型误设稳健的替代方案。

Method: 提出两种互补的贝叶斯程序：(1) 相干似然法——基于 Sverchkov 与 Pfeffermann (2004) 的恒等式，对给定研究变量下设计权重的样本级分布建模，反推条件包含概率；这是近期条件似然估计量的贝叶斯对应，在正确指定样本级联合模型下达到基于模型的效率界。(2) 稳健法——将同一权重模型仅作为 Neyman 正交化的设计加权得分的输入，并应用损失似然自助法；其可信区间在泊松抽样下渐近满足 sandwich 校正，权重模型误设时后验仍以真值为中心（但工作模型仍影响一阶方差）。参数化默认设定采用对总体级包含概率的 beta 回归，导出的样本级权重服从 beta prime 分布。第二种程序的数据自适应实现利用设计加权 Gamma/log-link 回归估计高效因子 $\bar\pi(x,y)=1/E_p(W\mid x,y)$，并在解析参数的联合一步损失似然自助法中使用，其有效性不要求对冗余拟合施加速率条件。

Result: 第一种方法在样本级联合模型正确指定时达到基于模型的效率界，但误设时可能产生偏差；第二种方法在泊松抽样下获得渐近 sandwich 校正的可信区间，对权重模型误设保持后验以真值为中心。模拟研究和加拿大劳动力（Canadian Workforce）数据验证了两种方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 两种贝叶斯方法形成互补：相干似然法在模型正确时效率最优，而基于 Neyman 正交化与损失似然自助法的稳健程序在权重模型误设下仍保持有效推断，且其数据自适应实现无需对冗余拟合施加速率条件。

Abstract: We develop two complementary Bayesian procedures for analytic inference under informative sampling. The first constructs a coherent likelihood by modeling the sample-level distribution of the design weight given the study variable and recovering the conditional inclusion probability through the identity of Sverchkov and Pfeffermann (2004); it is the Bayesian counterpart of a recently proposed conditional-likelihood estimator, attains the model-based efficiency bound under the correctly specified sample-level joint model, and can be biased otherwise. The second uses the same weight model only as input to a Neyman-orthogonalized analog of the design-weighted score and applies the loss-likelihood bootstrap; the resulting credible intervals are asymptotically sandwich-correct under Poisson sampling, and the posterior remains centered at the truth under misspecification of the weight model, although the working model can still affect the first-order variance. A beta regression on the population-level inclusion probability, inducing a beta prime sample-level weight distribution, serves as the parametric default. A data-adaptive implementation of the second procedure estimates the efficient factor $\barπ(x,y)=1/E_p(W\mid x,y)$ by a design-weighted Gamma/log-link regression and uses it in a joint one-step loss-likelihood bootstrap for the analytic parameter; its validity requires no rate conditions on the nuisance fit. We illustrate both procedures with simulations and Canadian Workforce data.

</details>


### [243] [Salient Bayesian Clustering for Proportional Data via a Multivariate Beta Mixture Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16494)
*Carmen B. Rodriguez,Briana JK Stephenson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯显著多元Beta混合模型（SAMBA-Mix），用于对邻域层面的社会健康决定因素（SDoH）比例数据进行聚类，并自动识别对聚类有贡献的关键变量，以揭示马萨诸塞州邻域的健康决定因素画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻域是跨越社会文化、经济和环境维度的多元实体。按SDoH对邻域聚类有助于公共卫生资源的合理配置与针对性干预。但邻域级SDoH数据是连续且有界（比例型）的，传统方法（如k-means、高斯混合模型）依赖欧氏距离或正态假设，难以适配；同时并非所有SDoH变量对刻画邻域聚类同等重要，纳入无关或弱信息变量会掩盖真实结构。因此需要一种既能灵活建模有界比例数据、又能识别相关变量的聚类方法。

Method: 提出贝叶斯显著多元Beta混合模型（SAMBA-Mix），利用多元Beta分布灵活建模有界比例数据，并嵌入特征显著性机制，使模型在聚类过程中自动识别对簇划分具有区分能力的关键变量，从而降低无关或弱信息变量的干扰。

Result: 与现有模型相比，SAMBA-Mix在聚类识别和结构解析方面表现出更高的准确性。基于美国医疗保健研究与质量局（AHRQ）SDoH数据库的应用，模型成功刻画了马萨诸塞州邻域层面的SDoH画像，并识别出最能区分不同画像的关键决定因素。

Conclusion: SAMBA-Mix能够有效处理有界比例型SDoH数据并纳入变量显著性筛选，在邻域聚类分析中性能优于现有方法，为基于社区需求的公共卫生资源分配和干预提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Neighborhoods are multifaceted entities whose attributes span sociocultural, economic, and environmental dimensions. Clustering neighborhoods based on social determinants of health (SDoH) can inform the allocation of public health resources and interventions tailored to community needs. Continuous and bounded data, as seen in aggregated neighborhood-level SDoH data, can pose challenges for widely used clustering methods (e.g., k-means or Gaussian mixture models) that rely on Euclidean distances or normality assumptions. Moreover, not all SDoH variables contribute equally to meaningfully characterize neighborhood clusters, and the inclusion of irrelevant or weakly informative determinants can obscure the underlying structure. To address this issue, we propose a Bayesian Salient Multivariate Beta mixture (SAMBA-Mix) model that flexibly models proportional data with embedded feature saliency to identify relevant variables for clustering. Our model showed improved accuracy in cluster identification and structure, compared to existing models. Using data from the U.S. Agency for Healthcare Research and Quality(AHRQ) SDoH database, we demonstrate the utility of our model to derive neighborhood-level SDoH profiles in Massachusetts and identify key determinants that best characterize differences amongst the different profiles.

</details>


### [244] [Locally calibrated and mesh-free inference for spatial point distributions: closed-form null, contamination law, and detectability threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16497)
*Henock Mwanza Lubukayi,Mechack Kabanga Ntolo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于经验贝叶斯Tweedie–Miyasawa恒等式的空间点分布局部推断方法，以替代蒙特卡洛校准；该方法利用高斯核下局部加权矩与尺度空间对数强度导数的关系，实现了无模拟、免网格、无自由参数的尺度分辨局部维数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间点分布的局部推断长期依赖蒙特卡洛校准，计算成本高；作者希望建立一种解析、免模拟且校准良好的替代方案，实现快速、可复现的尺度分辨局部结构检测与维数估计。

Method: 利用经验贝叶斯的Tweedie–Miyasawa恒等式，将高斯核下点分布的局部加权矩与对数强度在尺度空间的导数联系起来；首先证明刚性定理（该结构强制高斯核）；在完全空间随机下导出有界尺度间对比度的闭式零分布，得到无需模拟的逐点检验；进一步推导均匀背景中维数m、宽度w结构的污染律与显式可检测性阈值；据此构建无自由参数的尺度分辨局部维数估计器。

Result: 在标称水平0.05下测得的I类错误率为0.070，同一局部统计量的校准成本比蒙特卡洛低199倍；三维丝状结构的可检测性阈值为16π；在加州地震活动、三叶结及10071个SDSS星系上的应用显示，方法能在无空间网格下跨尺度分离局部结构，并复现已发表的宇宙网占比，且全部实验可由单一公开脚本复现。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Tweedie–Miyasawa恒等式的解析方法，能以远低于蒙特卡洛的成本实现校准良好、无网格、无自由参数的尺度分辨局部维数估计，并在模拟与真实天文数据上验证了有效性与可复现性。

Abstract: Local inference for spatial point distributions is dominated by Monte Carlo calibration. We develop an alternative based on the Tweedie--Miyasawa identities of empirical Bayes, which relate locally weighted moments of a point distribution under a Gaussian kernel to derivatives of its log-intensity in scale space. We first establish a rigidity theorem showing that the structure of these identities forces the Gaussian kernel. Under complete spatial randomness, we derive a closed-form null distribution for a bounded inter-scale contrast, yielding a calibrated simulation-free pointwise test. In experiments, the measured type I error is 0.070 at a nominal level of 0.05, with a calibration cost 199 times smaller than Monte Carlo for the same local statistic. We then derive a contamination law for structures of dimension m and width w embedded in a uniform background, together with an explicit detectability threshold. For a filament in three dimensions, the threshold is 16 pi. The resulting scale-resolved local dimension estimator has no free parameters. Applications to California seismicity, a trefoil knot, and 10,071 SDSS galaxies show that the method separates local structures across scales without a spatial mesh and reproduces published cosmic web fractions. All experiments are reproducible from a single public script.

</details>


### [245] [Causal Path Analysis from Perturbational and Population-Scale Single-Cell Data with Multiscale Confounding and Measurement Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16510)
*Kwangmoon Park,Hongzhe Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个整合单细胞扰动实验与群体规模单细胞研究的因果路径分析框架，通过外部学得的祖先关系约束网络拓扑，并在细胞与个体两个层面进行代理变量与变量误差校正，应用于急性髓系白血病识别调控通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞扰动实验提供基因调控的因果信息，而群体规模单细胞研究刻画人类群体中的基因表达与表型；二者互补但难以直接迁移。论文旨在解决如何将扰动网络迁移到目标群体等问题，同时处理多尺度单细胞测量中的潜在异质性与测量误差。

Method: 开发整合两类互补数据源的因果路径分析框架：不直接假设扰动基因网络可迁移到目标群体，而是利用外部学得的祖先关系约束网络拓扑，并从群体数据重新估计直接边与效应；针对细胞和个体两个层面的潜在异质性与测量误差，提出双层次代理变量（surrogate-variable）方法，并结合变量误差（errors-in-variables）校正用于网络回归与结局回归。

Result: 建立了关于混杂因子恢复以及网络与基因-结局效应高维估计的理论保证；模拟实验表明联合校正混杂与测量误差的重要性；在急性髓系白血病的应用中识别出连接转录调控因子与原始细胞计数的不同调控通路。

Conclusion: 该框架通过融合扰动与群体单细胞数据、结合代理变量与变量误差校正，能够实现可靠的因果路径分析，并在真实疾病数据中验证了其有效性，为跨尺度单细胞因果推断提供了理论与方法支持。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation experiments provide causal information on gene regulation, whereas population-scale single-cell studies characterize gene expression and phenotypes in human populations. We develop a framework that integrates these complementary data sources for causal path analysis. Rather than assuming that a perturbational gene network transfers directly to the target population, we use externally learned ancestral relationships to constrain the network topology and re-estimate its direct edges and effects from population data. To address latent heterogeneity and measurement error in multiscale single-cell measurements, we develop a surrogate-variable procedure operating at both the cell and subject levels, combined with errors-in-variables correction for network and outcome regressions. We establish theoretical guarantees for confounder recovery and high-dimensional estimation of network and gene-outcome effects. Simulations demonstrate the importance of jointly correcting confounding and measurement error. An application to acute myeloid leukemia identifies distinct regulatory pathways linking transcriptional regulators to blast count.

</details>


### [246] [Supervising the Chain Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16552)
*Stephan Marais,James Grove*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将链梯法的模式选择转化为有监督学习问题，通过在链梯损失函数上引入可解释的惩罚项与超参数，实现精算判断的声明式调整，并给出训练回路与验证分数，辅以真实 Schedule P 三角形的算例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链梯法的体积加权模式虽然在最小化某个显式损失函数，但实务中精算师往往不按该损失直接订定，而是对模式加以调整并记录最终调整后的比率。这种调整依赖主观判断，缺乏统一的、可声明、可复现的框架。作者希望把对模式的判断调整形式化为链梯损失函数上的一组定义明确的惩罚项与超参数，使判断可解释、可分类、可训练。

Method: 将链梯模式选择视为有监督学习：把链梯损失函数视作机器学习中的目标函数，并对其扩展。数据权重用衰减参数与幂参数推广，以体现近期性与体积加权；基准形状与平滑性通过参考惩罚与 Whittaker-Henderson 平滑引入；整体目标函数严格凸，可由单一线性系统最小化。每个超参数对应一种可解释的调整，分为经验调整与前瞻调整两类。经验调整可通过提出的训练回路，以留出日历对角线上的准备金验证分数更客观地设定。其余基于超参数的调整写成几乎处处可微的惩罚项，用于重定时或重塑模式。

Result: 给出了一个将链梯损失函数扩展为严格凸目标函数的统一框架，该目标可由单一线性系统求解；每个超参数均成为独立、可解释、可按判断声明并被归类为经验或前瞻调整的调整量。提出训练回路与准备金验证分数以客观设定经验调整。算例使用一个真实的 Schedule P 三角形，依次经历已发生（incurred）与已付（paid）两个训练阶段，演示了完整工作流程。

Conclusion: 通过将链梯模式选择纳入有监督学习框架并引入可解释惩罚与超参数，精算判断得以形式化为声明式调整，既保留判断空间又提升客观性与可复现性；所提出的严格凸目标函数、训练回路与验证分数为实务应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The chain ladder's volume-weighted pattern minimises an explicit loss function, yet is rarely booked as such. Practitioners adjust the pattern and record the final adjusted ratios. This paper treats the chain ladder's pattern selection as a supervised-learning problem. Judgement on pattern adjustments becomes a framework of defined penalties and hyperparameters on the chain ladder's loss function, treated here as an objective function in machine learning. Data weights are generalised with a decay and a power parameter for recency and volume weighting. Benchmark shaping and smoothness enter through a reference penalty and Whittaker-Henderson smoothing. The assembled objective is strictly convex and minimised by a single linear system. Each hyperparameter becomes an interpretable adjustment in its own right, declarable by judgement and categorised as an experience or a prospective adjustment. Experience adjustments can be set more objectively by a proposed training loop and a reserve validation score on held-out calendar diagonals. Further hyperparameter-based adjustments are written as almost-everywhere differentiable penalties that re-time or reshape the pattern. A worked example carries one real Schedule P triangle through an incurred and then a paid training stage, demonstrating the workflow.

</details>


### [247] [A Multiplicative Loss Function for Chain Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16561)
*James Grove,Stephan Marais*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于乘法误差结构的体积加权平方对数误差损失函数，其最小化由各进展期发展因子的体积加权几何平均实现。该估计量可从三条独立路径（损失最小化、准备金稳定性、对数正态极大似然）推导得出，并在362个Schedule P数据集的样本外研究中表现出优于传统链梯法体积加权平均的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代准备金模型日益依赖基于损失的估计，损失函数编码了假定的误差结构。Mack已证明链梯法的体积加权平均最小化加性的体积加权平方误差损失，但该损失隐含的是加法误差结构。本文认为实际发展过程更契合乘法误差结构，因此需要一个与之对应的损失函数及其最小化估计量，为链梯法和机器学习准备金模型提供更合理的备选方案。

Method: 作者在Mack的无分布（distribution-free）框架下，将加法误差替换为乘法误差结构，构造体积加权平方对数误差损失函数，证明其最小化解为各进展期个体发展因子的体积加权几何平均。随后通过两个独立论证验证同一估计量：一是对连续终极损失预测的稳定性要求，二是对数正态模型下的极大似然估计。最后以362个Schedule P公司-业务线数据集的样本外研究检验估计量的预测表现。

Result: 体积加权平方对数误差损失函数的最小化由体积加权几何平均实现；同一估计量同时满足准备金稳定性要求并经对数正态极大似然估计导出，从而获得损失最小化、准备金稳定性与参数似然三重佐证。样本外研究表明，该估计量相较体积加权平均在链梯法中提升了预测精度，并缓解了轻微的过度预测偏差。

Conclusion: 体积加权几何平均作为体积加权平均的替代估计量，具备更符合乘法误差结构的理论依据和多重（损失最小化、稳定性、似然）一致性论证。实证结果支持其在链梯法及其他基于损失的准备金模型（含机器学习方法）中应用，以提升预测准确性并纠正预测偏差。

Abstract: Reserving models increasingly rely on loss-based estimation, where the loss function encodes the assumed error structure. Mack demonstrated this for the chain ladder, showing that the volume-weighted average estimator minimises a volume-weighted squared-error loss function that is additive in successive claim developments. This paper instead considers a multiplicative error structure and proposes the corresponding volume-weighted squared log-error loss function. Adapting Mack's distribution-free framework, we show that this loss function is minimised by the volume-weighted geometric average of the individual development ratios. This provides practitioners with an alternative estimator of development ratios for chain-ladder-based models, and a candidate loss function for machine-learning-based reserving models. We further show that the same estimator arises from two independent arguments: a stability requirement on successive ultimate loss projections, and maximum-likelihood estimation under a log-normal model. The estimator thus admits three complementary justifications: loss minimisation, reserve stability, and parametric likelihood. An out-of-sample study of 362 Schedule P company-line datasets supports the use of the proposed estimator in place of the volume-weighted average for the chain ladder, by showing that it improves predictive accuracy and reduces a slight over-prediction bias.

</details>


### [248] [Statistical Inference for Bivariate Functional Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16562)
*Shreya Prakash,Fan Xia,Elena A. Erosheva*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文综述了函数型因果发现方法在理论、软件及应用中的统计保证，指出其缺乏统一推断框架，并提出了一个基于假设检验的双变量因果发现推断框架，通过重抽样估计四种因果发现结果的概率，提供不确定性量化和诊断洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数型因果发现方法依赖结构与分布假设来确定因果方向，但通常缺乏统计推断，导致方向性判定缺少不确定性量化与统一的推断框架，无法广泛适用于各类模型。

Method: 将因果方向判定重构为两个不相交的假设检验（拟合优度检验与独立性检验），对应四种因果发现结果；利用重抽样估计这些结果的概率（发生率）以提供额外推断层，并通过模拟与真实数据验证框架行为。

Result: 该检验框架为双变量因果发现提供显式的不确定性量化和对假设违背的诊断洞察，重抽样进一步估计因果发现结果的比率；模拟与真实数据应用展示了该框架在假设违背程度变化下的使用与表现。

Conclusion: 本文指出现有函数型因果发现方法缺乏统一推断框架，提出的基于假设检验的方法作为第一步填补该空白，并给出推进函数型因果发现统计保证的实践教训与建议。

Abstract: Causal discovery methods aim to determine the causal direction between variables using observational data. Functional causal discovery methods rely on structural and distributional assumptions to determine directionality but typically lack statistical inference. This paper reviews the statistical guarantees of existing functional causal discovery methods in theory, software, and applied use, highlighting a key gap: the absence of a unified inferential framework that applies broadly across model classes. As a first step toward addressing this gap, we formalize a test-based approach for bivariate causal discovery by repurposing goodness-of-fit and independence tests within a hypothesis-testing framework. Because directionality is determined through two disjoint hypothesis tests, corresponding to four causal discovery outcomes, the approach provides explicit uncertainty quantification and diagnostic insight into assumption violations. Resampling is further used to estimate the rates of causal discovery outcomes, offering an additional layer of inference. We demonstrate the use and behavior of our inferential framework through simulations that vary the degree of assumption violation, as well as through real-data applications. We conclude with practical lessons and recommendations for advancing statistical guarantees in functional causal discovery.

</details>


### [249] [A Multiverse of Good and Bad Controls: Candidate Causal Graphs for Interpreting Model Robustness Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16618)
*Shoki Okubo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于候选因果图（DAG）的模型稳健性分析框架，将多宇宙（multiverse）中的调整集按其所服从的因果图分组，利用有限混合恒等式把总离散度分解为“图内”与“图间”成分，以图间占比作为条件描述性度量，避免把混杂调整集与中介/对撞调整集混同合并所带来的误导性稳健性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多宇宙稳健性分析会把用于识别目标估计量（estmand）的有效控制集，与以中介或对撞子为条件的无效控制集混合汇总，导致效应估计跨规格的离散度被污染，产生误导性的稳健性判定。作者希望将研究者关于争议控制变量的对立假设显式化为少量候选因果图，并在每个图内部评估稳健性，从而区分“所有图下都脆弱”与“仅由非法规格引发的表面不稳定”。

Method: 首先将关于争议控制变量的竞争性假设刻画为一小组候选因果图（candidate DAGs），枚举每个图所许可的调整集（adjustment sets）。然后基于有限混合（finite-mixture）恒等式，把许可多宇宙的总离散度分解为图内离散（within-graph）与图间离散（between-graph）两个成分；图间占比构成一个条件描述性汇总统计量，其解释依赖于候选图集合、权重以及是否存在共同的目标估计量。在得到每个图内的稳健性度量后，再跨图汇总报告条件于各图的稳健性指标。同时提供 R 包实现完整工作流。

Result: 模拟研究表明，池化（pooled）的稳健性评估可能具有误导性，而分解方法也有其适用边界；对飓风死亡、职业培训与工会工资三个应用案例的分析显示：（1）有些脆弱性在所有因果图下都持续存在；（2）有些不稳定性完全来自未被任何图许可的（非法）规格；（3）某些被判定为“脆弱”的结论，实际上在每个经调整集识别的候选世界中都隐藏着显著的效应溢价。

Conclusion: 通过在候选因果图层面显式声明对争议控制变量的假设，并将多宇宙总离散度分解为图内与图间成分，研究者可以获得更可靠、更可解释的稳健性判定，避免池化评估将“非法规格引发的不稳定”误读为实质性脆弱；配套 R 包为该方法提供了可复现的实现。

Abstract: Model robustness analysis estimates an effect across a multiverse of specifications that pools control sets identifying the declared estimand with sets that condition on mediators or colliders. We propose stating rival assumptions about contested controls as a small set of candidate causal graphs, enumerating the adjustment sets each graph licenses, and reporting robustness metrics conditional on each graph. A finite-mixture identity splits the licensed multiverse's dispersion into within-graph and between-graph components; the between-graph share is a conditional descriptive summary whose reading depends on the candidate set, the weights, and a common estimand. Simulations examine misleading pooled robustness assessments and the limits of the decomposition. Applications to hurricane fatalities, job training, and union wages show fragility that survives every graph, instability produced by unlicensed specifications, and a fragility verdict concealing a significant premium in each adjustment-identified candidate world. An R package implements the workflow.

</details>


### [250] [A flexible framework for treatment effect inference in longitudinal clinical studies with skewed outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16670)
*Kazushi Maruo,Ryota Ishii,Yusuke Yamaguchi,Toshio Shimokawa,Tomoyuki Sugimoto,Masahiko Gosho*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出 Box–Cox 多元回归（BCMVR）框架，用于纵向连续偏态结局数据的分析。相比假定各组、各时点共享同一变换参数的 BCMMRM，BCMVR 允许变换参数随组和时间变化，从而在分布形状存在异质性时得到更稳健的治疗效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向连续结局常用正态假设下的 MMRM 分析，但临床结局常呈偏态，导致基于均值的治疗效应难以解释且统计效率降低。现有 BCMMRM 虽可通过逆变换进行基于中位数的推断，但其假定各组与各时点共用同一 Box–Cox 变换参数；当分布形状因组别或时间而异、或治疗同时影响集中趋势与分布尾部形态时，该假定会导致治疗效应估计有偏，且单一位置摘要（如中位数）不足以刻画疗效。

Method: 提出 BCMVR（Box–Cox 多元回归）框架，允许变换参数随治疗组与时间点灵活变化。在此基础上，提供两类可解释的推断：(1) 中位数差；(2) 基于概率的治疗效应量度，即随机选取的一名治疗组患者结局优于对照组患者的概率，该指标整合整个结局分布的信息。通过模拟研究评估 BCMMRM 与 BCMVR 在不同分布形状下的估计偏倚及概率指标的表现。

Result: 模拟研究表明，当各组分布形状不同时，BCMMRM 会产生有偏估计，而 BCMVR 提供近似无偏的估计。基于概率的治疗效应量度在稳健性与统计效率之间取得良好平衡，可作为分布形状差异时的补充摘要。

Conclusion: BCMVR 框架为分布异质性条件下的治疗效应推断提供了一种灵活且可解释的方法，其概率型效应量度能够更全面地刻画治疗效应。

Abstract: Longitudinal continuous outcomes in clinical trials are commonly analyzed using mixed models for repeated measures (MMRM) under normality assumptions. However, many clinical outcomes are skewed, making mean-based treatment effects difficult to interpret and potentially reducing statistical efficiency. The Box--Cox MMRM (BCMMRM) approach accommodates skewness by enabling inference on model-based median differences via inverse transformation. However, BCMMRM typically assumes a common transformation parameter across treatment groups and time points. When distributional shapes differ between groups or evolve over time, this assumption may lead to biased treatment effect. Furthermore, when treatment affects not only central tendency but also distributional shape or tail behavior, treatment effects may not be adequately characterized by a single location summary such as the median.
  We propose the Box--Cox multivariate regression (BCMVR) framework for longitudinal data with skewed outcomes. BCMVR relaxes this restriction by allowing transformation parameters to vary across groups and time points. The framework enables inference based on interpretable summaries, including median differences and a probability-based treatment effect quantifying the probability that a randomly selected patient in one group has a better outcome than one in another group. This measure integrates information over the entire outcome distribution and provides a complementary summary when distributional shapes differ. Simulation studies demonstrate that BCMMRM can produce biased estimates when distributions differ in shape, whereas BCMVR provides nearly unbiased estimation. The probability-based measure achieves a favorable balance between robustness and statistical efficiency. The proposed framework provides a flexible and interpretable approach to treatment effect inference under distributional heterogeneity.

</details>


### [251] [Equitable Partition Realizability for Dynamics-preserving and Privacy-aware Network Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16762)
*Riccardo Porcedda*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究图的"度序列可实现性"的自然推广——由公平划分（EP）诱导的EP可实现性问题，并将其应用于从隐私保护的部分信息中重构网络，实现可调的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配置模型的组合基础是度序列可实现性，但仅靠度约束无法保留图动力学特性，导致配置模型难以恢复与度序列不强相关的中介中心性等中心性度量，因此需要引入由公平划分诱导的更精细的可实现性问题。

Method: 作者定义EP可实现性问题：给定图的公平划分（EP），判断并构造实现该划分的简单无向无环图；通过将其归约为与Havel–Hakimi和Gale–Ryser定理相关的子问题来求解。进一步提出近似公平划分（ε-EP）下的求解方法，支持从部分且更隐私保护的信息重构网络；并以边重叠（edge overlap）评估隐私性，为ε-EP可实现性方案推导了该指标的预测器。

Result: 在Karate、Cora、CiteSeer和PubMed数据集上的实验表明，该算法相比Havel–Hakimi算法、Newman配置模型和随机块模型，取得了有利且可调的隐私-效用权衡；在真实数据和随机图上均表现出关于边数的近似线性时间复杂度。

Conclusion: EP可实现性及ε-EP可实现性为从公平划分信息重构网络提供了新框架，能够在隐私保护与图结构/中心性效用之间实现可调权衡，且算法高效、近似线性扩展。

Abstract: Degree-sequence realizability is the combinatorial basis of configuration models, but degree constraints alone do not ensure the preservation of graph dynamics. Hence, configuration models are unable to recover centrality measures, unless these are strongly correlated with the degree sequence. To address this matter, we introduce EP-realizability, the analogue problem induced by an equitable partition (EP): given the EP of a graph, decide whether the partition is realized by a simple undirected loopless graph and therefore construct such a graph. After defining the problem, we solve it by reducing it to sub-problems related to Havel--Hakimi and the Gale--Ryser theorem. We also face the challenge of solving the problem with an Approximate Equitable Partition ($\varepsilon$-EP), so that it is possible to reconstruct a network starting from partial and more privacy-preserving information. We evaluate privacy with edge overlap, deriving also, for our proposed $\varepsilon$-EP-realizability solution, a predictor for this metric. Experiments on Karate, Cora, CiteSeer and PubMed datasets show that our algorithm achieves a favourable and tunable privacy--utility trade-off, comparing the results with Havel--Hakimi algorithm, Newman's configuration model and a stochastic block model. Finally, both with real data and random graphs, we show that our algorithm has approximately linear time complexity with respect to the number of edges.

</details>


### [252] [Causal Discovery via Transformed Low-Rank Quantile Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16931)
*Ryo Kamimura,Thong Pham*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出低秩分位数曲面（LRQS）二元因果模型，通过条件分位数曲面的低秩函数分解来判定因果方向，超越传统位置-尺度及后非线性异方差模型的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二元因果模型（如位置-尺度噪声模型、后非线性异方差噪声模型）仅能刻画有限的条件分布形状变化，无法处理具有高阶分布形状变异与强非线性观测失真的因果机制，需要更通用且可辨识的模型。

Method: 提出 LRQS 模型：在因果方向上，条件分位数曲面经未知单调变换后具有低秩函数分解结构。通过交替进行离散化分位数曲面的秩约束逼近与未知单调变换的保序估计的非参数拟合过程，构造简单而有效的因果得分。

Result: 证明 LRQS 的通用可辨识性：因果方向上变换后的分位数曲面是低秩的，而在对应约束下的反向可表示性仅对微调（fine-tuned）的特殊原因边缘分布才可能成立。在具有高阶秩分布形状变化与强非线性失真的合成机制及标准二元基准上，LRQS 表现出色。

Conclusion: LRQS 作为通用且可辨识的二元因果模型，能有效刻画条件分布形状与观测失真超出位置-尺度假设的情形，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We propose Low-Rank Quantile Surfaces (LRQS), a bivariate causal model in which, in the causal direction, an unknown monotone transformation of the conditional quantile surface admits a low-rank functional decomposition. LRQS subsumes location-scale noise models and post-nonlinear heteroscedastic noise models, while allowing multiple quantile bases to represent changes beyond location-scale effects. We prove generic identifiability of LRQS: the transformed quantile surface is low rank in the causal direction, whereas reverse representability under the corresponding constraints occurs only for exceptional, fine-tuned cause marginals. We provide a simple-yet-powerful causal score using a nonparametric fitting procedure that alternates between rank-constrained approximation of discretized quantile surfaces and isotonic estimation of the unknown monotone transformation. Experiments on synthetic mechanisms with higher-rank distributional shape variation and strong nonlinear distortions, together with standard bivariate benchmarks, show that LRQS is especially effective when conditional distributional shape or observation distortion goes beyond existing location-scale assumptions.

</details>


### [253] [Covariate Selection for Doubly Robust Double/debiased Machine Learning Estimators for Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17238)
*Muwon Kwon,Peter M. Steiner*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维数据中的因果效应估计，本文提出将倾向得分模型与结果模型所选协变量的并集用于重新估计两个模型，以增强双重稳健（DR）估计量在机器学习协变量选择下的实用性；模拟显示并集方法比单独所选协变量集更能减少混杂偏误，且后Lasso优于标准Lasso。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据给因果效应估计带来挑战，因为难以识别正确设定模型所需的协变量。双重/去偏机器学习（DML）虽能缓解正则化和过拟合偏误，但对机器学习协变量选择与双重稳健（DR）性质之间关系关注不足。机器学习协变量选择可能导致差异化的协变量选择或两个模型同时设定错误，从而限制DR性质的实际效用。

Method: 提出使用倾向得分（PS）机器学习模型与结果机器学习模型所选协变量的并集，来重新估计两个模型。通过模拟研究比较了并集方法与使用各自独立所选协变量集的方法，并对比了机器学习估计与常规DR估计，以及标准Lasso与后Lasso（post-Lasso）的表现。

Result: 模拟结果表明，使用并集一致地比使用单独所选协变量集减少更多的混杂偏误；机器学习估计即使在有利于Lasso的条件下也未一致优于常规DR估计；后Lasso比标准Lasso减少更多混杂偏误。

Conclusion: 成功地将机器学习用于因果推断不仅取决于机器学习算法本身，还取决于如何将通过协变量选择获得的信息纳入因果效应估计。提出使用两个模型所选协变量的并集重估模型，是一种提升DR估计量在高维场景下实用价值的有效策略。

Abstract: High-dimensional data create challenges for causal effect estimation because identifying the covariates needed for correct model specification becomes increasingly difficult. Double/debiased machine learning (DML) facilitates the use of machine learning (ML) for causal inference by mitigating regularization and overfitting bias, but comparatively less attention has been given to covariate selection in relation to the double robustness (DR) property possessed by some DML estimators. In particular, ML-based covariate selection may result in differential covariate selection or in misspecification of both models, thereby limiting the practical utility of the DR property. To address these issues, we propose using the union of the covariates selected by the propensity score (PS) and outcome ML models to re-estimate both models. Simulation results show that using the union consistently reduces more confounding bias than using separate selected covariate sets. The results also show that ML-based estimation does not uniformly outperform conventional DR estimation, even under conditions favorable to the Lasso, and that post-Lasso reduces more confounding bias than standard Lasso. These findings demonstrate that successful use of ML for causal inference depends not only on the ML algorithm but also on how the information obtained through covariate selection is incorporated into causal effect estimation.

</details>


### [254] [Conformal Policy Learning with Distribution-Free Safety Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17296)
*Ying Jin,Naoki Egami*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出共形策略学习（CPL），一种具备有限样本、分布无关安全保证的策略学习方法，通过控制对"相对对照会受伤害"的个体施加处理的概率来贯彻"不伤害"原则，并在估计一致时实现受安全约束的渐近最优福利，且可扩展到观测研究中的双重稳健保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学、公共政策等高风险场景中，仅提升平均结果并不足够，决策者还需保护个体免受伤害（希波克拉底"不伤害"原则）。现有策略学习方法主要关注平均福利最大化，缺乏对个体层面伤害的显式、有限样本、且不依赖结果模型假设的安全保证。

Method: CPL将每个治疗决策视为对"反事实伤害"假设的检验，通过对共形p值进行阈值化来决定是否施加处理。由于处理与对照的潜在结果无法同时观测，CPL利用可观测代理与选择性校准（selective calibration）来构造p值。在随机实验中，CPL依赖标准可交换性条件提供有限样本安全保证；在观测研究中，采用learn-then-balance权重实现双重稳健的安全保证。

Result: 在随机实验的可交换性条件下，CPL在用户指定水平上提供有限样本安全保证，且无需任何结果建模假设；当结果模型被一致估计时，CPL在安全约束下实现渐近最优福利；在观测研究中通过learn-then-balance权重获得双重稳健安全保证。大量仿真与一项旨在减少阴谋论信念的AI干预实证研究验证了CPL的有效性。

Conclusion: CPL为策略学习引入了一种新的分布无关安全保证，能够在避免个体伤害的同时兼顾平均福利，兼具有限样本理论与渐近最优性，并在真实数据上表现出良好的实用前景。

Abstract: Policy learning aims to determine who should be treated based on individual characteristics. In high-stakes settings such as medicine and public policy where safety is a central concern, improving the average outcomes alone may not be sufficient: decision makers may also seek to protect individuals from harm, in line with the Hippocratic principle of ``do no harm.'' In this paper, we propose \textit{conformal policy learning} (CPL), a policy learning procedure with a new distribution-free safety guarantee that controls the probability of assigning treatment to an individual who would be harmed relative to control. CPL views each treatment decision as testing a hypothesis of counterfactual harm and assigns treatment by thresholding conformal p-values. These p-values use observable proxies and selective calibration to address the challenge that the potential outcomes under comparison are never simultaneously observed. For randomized experiments, under standard exchangeability conditions, CPL provides finite-sample safety guarantee at a user-specified level, without imposing any outcome modeling assumptions. Moreover, when the outcome model is consistently estimated, CPL achieves asymptotically optimal welfare subject to the safety constraint. In observational studies, CPL with learn-then-balance weights achieves doubly robust safety guarantees. We evaluate CPL through extensive simulations and apply it to an empirical study of AI-powered interventions designed to reduce conspiracy beliefs.

</details>


### [255] [First-Crossing Reduction and Inference for No-Rescue Effects under Deterministic Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17495)
*Shu Tamano*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究急救用药（rescue medication）方案中的部分识别问题，针对"若不使用急救药会怎样"这一反事实结局，给出尖锐（sharp）的部分识别边界与置信区间构造方法，核心是把边界问题归结为对"首次越界状态"上条件均值效应的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验方案常规定当患者状况越过预设阈值时须给予急救用药，导致越过阈值后"持续不急救"的结局无法观测，若无外推假设则无法点识别。已有方法往往需要建模越界后轨迹或作出外推，本文希望在保留"原不急救干预"的前提下，仅用跨层均值的限制条件得到不依赖外推的识别与推断。

Method: 作者证明该均值的边界可精确归约为对"首次越界历史（first-crossing histories）"下暂停急救的条件均值效应的限制。基于此表示，对预先指定的越界层（crossing-stratum）均值施加限制，得到三项成果：(i) 一个有限凸规划给出无需对越界后轨迹建模的尖锐边界，且可由保留观测分布的完全数据律实现；(ii) 置信区间纳入抽样不确定性与数值误差，覆盖整个尖锐集合，包括无可观测越界的层；(iii) 在局部可行性条件下，即使层概率为零，抽样与数值误差也能控制端点精度，而有效覆盖则无需此条件。

Result: 实验表明，在样本量较大时，基于层均值限制的置信区间可以更窄；在急救事件罕见的情况下，计入空层的不确定性能够维持有效覆盖。

Conclusion: 该方法通过跨层均值限制即可实现尖锐的部分识别与有效的区间推断，避免了对越界后轨迹的建模与外推，并能在稀疏（甚至零概率）层情形下保持统计推断的有效性和数值稳健性。

Abstract: Clinical protocols may require rescue medication when a patient's condition crosses a prespecified threshold. The outcome under continued non-rescue is then unobserved after first crossing, preventing point identification without extrapolation assumptions. In this paper, we develop sharp partial identification and inference for this hypothetical estimand while retaining the original no-rescue intervention. We show that bounding this mean reduces exactly to restricting conditional mean effects of withholding rescue at first-crossing histories. Building on this representation, restrictions on prespecified crossing-stratum averages yield three results. (i) A finite convex program gives sharp bounds without modeling post-crossing trajectories, attained by full-data laws that preserve the observed distribution. (ii) Confidence intervals incorporate sampling uncertainty and numerical error to cover the entire sharp set, including strata with no observed crossings. (iii) Under local feasibility conditions, sampling and numerical errors control endpoint accuracy even with zero-probability strata, whereas valid coverage requires no such conditions. Our experiments show that the stratum-average restrictions can yield narrower confidence intervals in larger samples, and that accounting for uncertainty in empty strata maintains valid coverage when rescue is rare.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Copula Adapted Directed Acyclic Graph for Cluster Representation of Biomedical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16240)
*Heranga K. Rathnasekara,Norou Diawara,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 CopDAG 框架，将 copula 模型的非高斯、非线性依赖建模与基于 DAG 的因果结构发现集成相结合，生成对聚类友好的数据表示，无需标签即可在 16 个生物医学数据集上取得最优聚类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学领域普遍存在诊断错误和标签错误，损害了预测模型和数据驱动结果的可靠性。传统的无监督聚类方法假设限制性的数据分布，难以捕捉高维生物医学数据中的复杂依赖关系；而监督学习又依赖难以获取的准确标签。因此需要一种不依赖数据标注、直接从特征间复杂关系出发对数据进行分层的聚类友好表示方法。

Method: 提出 CopDAG（copula-adapted DAG）数据表示框架：1) 利用 copula 模型刻画特征间的非高斯、非线性和非对称依赖，放宽多元正态性、线性依赖与对称关系的假设；2) 采用基于有向无环图（DAG）的多种因果结构发现（CSD）方法组成的集成，识别特征间稳定的因果关系；3) 将二者结合生成新的数据表示，再用 K-means 进行聚类，直接从特征关系中预测真实类别标签。

Result: 在 16 个生物医学数据集上，CopDAG 生成的表示经 K-means 聚类后，在归一化聚类准确率和调整兰德指数（ARI）两项指标上均排名第一，优于其他 12 种对比方法。

Conclusion: CopDAG 能够在不使用数据标注和无需监督学习的情况下，直接从特征间关系预测真实类别标签，同时提供聚类可视化结果和可解释的生物医学特征因果结构，为生物医学数据的无标签分层提供了有效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Diagnostic errors and mislabeling are common in biomedicine, which compromise the reliability of predictive models and data-driven outcomes. Stratifying unlabeled biomedical data based on complex relationships between features eliminates the need for data labels and overcomes the limitations of supervised learning. Traditional clustering methods assume restrictive data distributions, making them suboptimal for capturing complex dependencies in high-dimensional biomedical data. This paper introduces a novel cluster-friendly data presentation framework that integrates the non-Gaussian and non-linear feature dependence of copula models with an ensemble of causal structure discovery (CSD) methods based on Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs). While copulas model flexible multivariate distributions by relaxing assumptions related to multivariate normality, linear dependence, and symmetric relationships, an ensemble of DAG-based CSD methods identifies stable causal relationships between features. When clustered using K-means, the new data representation obtained by the proposed copula-adapted DAG (CopDAG) ranks first among the 12 methods in normalized clustering accuracy and adjusted Rand index across 16 biomedical datasets. Our CopDAG method predicts ground-truth class labels directly from feature relationships without data annotations and supervised learning, while also providing cluster visualizations and explainable causal structures of the biomedical data features.

</details>


### [257] [Compute-Optimal Pretrain--Fine-tune in Ridge Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16262)
*Alex Buna,Fanghui Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在固定总优化预算的两阶段预训练-微调框架下，将计算分配问题形式化为"计算切分"问题，以梯度下降训练的岭回归最小二乘模型为载体，刻画了数据依赖评估几何结构下的最优切分，并揭示其由预训练与微调经验协方差的预测相关谱分量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练后微调的范式存在一个计算分配难题：在固定训练预算下，用于改进上游（预训练）目标的计算会挤占下游适配可用的计算。尽管这一问题在实践中很重要，但即便在简单模型中其理论上也未被充分理解，因此本文旨在对这一权衡给出理论刻画。

Method: 作者将计算分配建模为两阶段预训练-微调流程下的"计算切分"问题，并固定总优化预算；以梯度下降训练的岭回归最小二乘（regularised least squares）作为可解析的设定，借助微调问题所诱导的、依赖数据的评估几何结构来刻画最优切分。技术层面，分析依赖于基不变的、特征空间级别的谱分解，以及对不互易的预训练与微调动力学施加微扰控制。

Result: 结果表明，最优计算切分取决于预训练方向如何影响微调预测，以及微调位移如何通过下游数据几何被观察；具体而言，相关量由预训练与微调经验协方差中"与预测相关"的谱分量决定。

Conclusion: 本文在可解的最小二乘设定下，首次对预训练-微调的计算分配权衡给出了依赖数据几何的理论刻画，将最优切分归结于两类经验协方差的预测相关谱分量，并通过基不变的谱分解与非互易动力学的微扰控制完成证明。

Abstract: Pretraining followed by fine-tuning introduces a compute-allocation problem: under a fixed training budget, compute spent improving the upstream objective reduces the compute available for downstream adaptation. Despite its practical importance, this trade-off is not yet well understood theoretically, even in simple models. In this paper, we cast this allocation as a compute-split problem under a two-stage pretrain--fine-tune procedure with fixed total optimisation budget, using regularised least squares trained by gradient descent as a tractable setting. We characterise the optimal split under data-dependent evaluation geometries induced by the fine-tuning problem. Our results show that the allocation depends on how pretraining directions affect fine-tuning predictions and how fine-tuning shifts are seen through downstream data geometry. In particular, the relevant quantities are determined by prediction-relevant spectral components of the pretraining and fine-tuning empirical covariances. Technically, the analysis relies on a basis-invariant, eigenspace-level spectral decomposition, together with perturbative control of the non-commuting pretraining and fine-tuning dynamics.

</details>


### [258] [Mini-batch Sampling Strategies for Long-Tailed Image Classification: An Empirical Study on CIFAR-100-LT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16365)
*Siyu Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了长尾图像分类中四种小批量采样策略（均匀实例采样、类别平衡采样、平方根采样、渐进平衡采样），在统一偏置-方差框架下分析其对梯度估计的影响，并通过CIFAR-100-LT实验揭示渐进平衡采样对尾部类别的稳健提升及类别平衡采样可能导致的过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集常呈长尾分布，小批量（mini-batch）的组成由采样策略决定，直接影响随机梯度估计，进而影响收敛行为和全类别谱上的泛化性能。现有研究对各类采样策略缺乏系统性的理论统一与公平实证对比，难以判断何时、如何重平衡才真正有益，尤其是对稀有尾部类别。

Method: 提出统一的偏置-方差框架，刻画四种采样策略（均匀实例采样、类别平衡采样、平方根采样、渐进平衡采样）对梯度估计的影响，暴露"无偏优化经验损失"与"稀有类别公平表示"之间的张力。实验采用ResNet-32在CIFAR-100-LT上，设置三种不平衡比率（ρ=10,50,100），每种策略在同一随机种子内共享相同的长尾子集与初始化，进行受控公平比较。

Result: 在ρ=100时，渐进采样使尾部类别准确率相对均匀基线提升25%（13.5% vs 10.8%），且在三个种子间一致；但总体准确率与均匀采样在种子间波动下无法区分（40.0% vs 39.7%），说明尾部增益才是稳健效应。类别平衡采样在ρ=100时对所有类别组（包括其旨在帮助的尾部类别）准确率均下降，归因于对稀缺数据的极端过采样导致过拟合；在ρ=50时该失效仅局限于头部和中部类别。结果表明"何时进行重平衡"与"重平衡多少"同等重要。

Conclusion: 采样策略对小批量组成及梯度估计的影响可通过统一偏置-方差框架理解。渐进平衡采样能稳健提升尾部类别准确率且不损害总体精度，而类别平衡采样在极端不平衡下反而因过拟合伤害尾部类别。重平衡的时机与强度同样关键，为长尾分类的采样策略选择提供了理论依据与实验指导。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often exhibit long-tailed class distributions, where a few head classes contain a large number of training samples while a large number of tail classes have only a few. The composition of each mini-batch, determined by the sampling strategy, governs which classes contribute to the stochastic gradient estimate, and therefore affects convergence behaviour and generalisation across the whole class spectrum. We provide a systematic theoretical and empirical comparison of four mini-batch sampling strategies for long-tailed image classification: uniform instance sampling, class-balanced sampling, square-root sampling, and progressively balanced sampling. We place all four in a unified bias-variance framework describing their effect on gradient estimation, which exposes the tension between unbiased optimisation of the empirical loss and fair representation of rare classes. We then evaluate them under controlled conditions using ResNet-32 on CIFAR-100-LT at three imbalance ratios (rho = 10, 50, 100), with every strategy sharing the same long-tailed subsets and initialisation within a seed. Progressive sampling improves tail-class accuracy by 25% relative to the uniform baseline at rho = 100 (13.5% versus 10.8%), consistently across all three seeds, while its overall accuracy is not distinguishable from that of uniform sampling given the seed-to-seed variation (40.0% versus 39.7%); the tail-class gain, not the overall gain, is the robust effect. At rho = 100, class-balanced sampling degrades accuracy on every class group, including the tail classes it is designed to help, which we attribute to overfitting caused by extreme oversampling of scarce data; at rho = 50 this failure is confined to head and medium classes. These results indicate that when rebalancing is applied during training matters as much as how much rebalancing is applied.

</details>


### [259] [Learned Look-Ahead Splitting Rule for CART](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16440)
*Andrew Gao,Tianlin Liu,Ruichen Han,Lu Tian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种前瞻（look-ahead）的决策树构建方法，通过评估候选分裂在生长一棵常规CART子树后所能实现的预测误差下降来选择分裂，并设计一种"智能前瞻"算法以降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CART采用贪婪分裂规则，仅最大化每个节点的即时预测误差下降，计算高效但可能错过那些短期收益小、经后续划分后能带来显著下游改善的分裂，尤其在层级结构或交互效应驱动的数据中。

Method: 提出前瞻树构建方法：对每个候选分裂，以其下生长一棵常规CART子树后所实现的预测误差减少量作为评价标准。为缓解全前瞻计算开销大的问题，提出智能前瞻算法，利用节点级特征学习下游分裂值。整个框架保持递归划分的可解释性。

Result: 通过模拟研究在多种设定下比较常规方法、全前瞻方法与智能前瞻方法，并将所提方法应用于两个真实数据实例，结果显示新方法具有优势。

Conclusion: 前瞻式分裂选择能改善层级或交互驱动情形下的树模型性能，智能前瞻算法在保持可解释性的同时显著降低计算负担，模拟与实证均验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Classification and regression trees are typically constructed using a greedy splitting rule that maximizes the immediate reduction in prediction error at each node. Although this strategy is computationally efficient, it can miss splits that yield small short-term gains but create substantial downstream improvements after further partitioning. We propose a look-ahead tree-building method that evaluates each candidate split by the prediction error reduction achieved after growing a conventional CART subtree below that split. Because the full look-ahead procedure can be computationally expensive, we also describe a smart look-ahead algorithm that learns downstream split values using node-level features. The proposed framework preserves the interpretability of recursive partitioning while improving split selection in hierarchical or interaction-driven settings. We conduct a simulation study comparing conventional, full look-ahead, and smart look-ahead methods under several settings and apply the proposed methods to analyze two real data examples demonstrating the merit of the new methods.

</details>


### [260] [Certified Inference and Training for Deep Equilibrium Networks: A Continuation Framework with Polynomial Complexity Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16485)
*Alex Borisevich*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个经认证的延续法框架，用于均衡点计算与深度均衡网络（DEQ）训练，将训练形式化为插值到精度 2^{-b}，并给出了关于比特代价、训练趟数与通道数的可证明保证，核心部分由 Lean 4 形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度均衡网络和均衡点计算涉及隐式非线性方程组，传统求解与训练缺乏严格的数值与复杂度保证。作者希望为 DEQ 的推理和训练提供端到端的认证（certified）框架，明确唯一分支选择、误差边界、计算复杂度等，以克服黑箱性并保证可靠性。

Method: 推理端采用紧凑输入同伦（compact input homotopy）从给定起始根选取唯一分支，并用带舍入的牛顿追踪器（rounded Newton tracker）在受认证的边界、条件数、导数与管道半径（tube-radius）界内追踪该分支。训练端将训练表述为插值到精度 2^{-b}，在局部加低秩递推中引入可编程的休眠双线性秩一通道（dormant bilinear rank-one channels）；通过加载 Tikhonov 求解（loaded Tikhonov solves）在不做谱分解的情况下诊断失败的插值趟，并用与趟残差对齐的输出保持修复方向来补足。整个训练建立在经认证的门实现、每趟区域的列稳定性、良态推理及有限更新误差预算之上。复杂度分析通过多项式几何、编码、精度与后端预算给出，并用 Lean 4 验证量化核心与具体推理后端。

Result: 在满足认证承诺类（promise class）的前提下，推理与训练均以 O(poly(L+b)) 比特代价完成（L 为编码实例长度，2^{-b} 为精度）。训练器使用 O(b+ℓ) 趟（passes），并从以 2^ℓ 为界的初始残差出发预留通道；数值比较展示了加载机制的效用。

Conclusion: 作者提出了首个在推理与训练两端都有端到端比特复杂度保证且经形式化验证的 DEQ 框架，在特定承诺类上实现了可证明的高效均衡计算与训练，为可靠深度学习及数值计算提供了理论与实现基础。

Abstract: We develop a certified continuation framework for equilibrium computation and for training deep equilibrium networks (DEQs), with training formulated as interpolation to accuracy $2^{-b}$. For inference, compact input homotopy selects a unique branch from a supplied start root, and a rounded Newton tracker follows it under certified boundary, conditioning, derivative, and tube-radius bounds. For training, we augment local-plus-low-rank recurrence with programmable dormant bilinear rank-one channels. Loaded Tikhonov solves diagnose a failed interpolation pass without spectral decomposition; an output-preserving repair aligned with the pass residual supplies the required direction. Training requires certified gate realization and column stability on each pass region, well-posed inference, and finite-update error budgets. With polynomial geometric, encoding, precision, and complete backend budgets, both certified inference and training have bit cost $O(\operatorname{poly}(L+b))$, where $L$ is the encoded instance length. The trainer uses $O(b+\ell)$ passes and reserve channels from an initial residual bounded by $2^\ell$. These guarantees concern a certified promise class. Lean 4 verifies the quantitative core and concrete inference backend; numerical comparisons illustrate the loaded mechanism.

</details>


### [261] [Time-warping estimation via stationarity-based learning of the de-warped signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16796)
*Corentin Presvôts,Adrien Meynard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可训练的时间扭曲估计模型 TWET，将时间扭曲估计转化为小波域的平稳化问题，采用层级空洞卷积架构，并引入可微平稳性准则进行端到端优化，在提升变形重建精度的同时大幅缩短计算时间，适用于低延迟场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间扭曲估计是信号处理中的基础问题，广泛应用于生物声学、雷达和生物医学分析等领域。现有方法在变形重建精度与计算效率上存在不足，难以兼顾低延迟应用需求，因此需要一种精度更高、速度更快的时间扭曲估计方法。

Method: 提出 Time-Warping Estimation Trainable（TWET）模型。核心思路是将时间扭曲估计建模为小波域的平稳化问题，利用层级空洞卷积（hierarchical dilated convolutional）架构从单一观测中估计时间扭曲函数，并设计可微的平稳性准则（differentiable stationarity criterion）以实现端到端优化。

Result: 与现有方法对比，TWET 在变形重建精度上表现更优，同时计算时间显著降低，使其能够满足低延迟应用的要求。

Conclusion: TWET 通过小波域平稳化建模、层级空洞卷积与可微平稳性准则，实现了高精度且高效的时间扭曲估计，验证了该框架在低延迟应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Time-warping estimation is a fundamental problem in signal processing with applications in bioacoustics, radar, and biomedical analysis. This paper introduces a Time-Warping Estimation Trainable (TWET) model for estimating timewarping functions from a single observation. The proposed approach formulates time-warping estimation as a stationarization problem in the wavelet domain and leverages a hierarchical dilated convolutional architecture to estimate the time-warping functions. A differentiable stationarity criterion is introduced for end-to-end optimization. TWET is compared with existing approaches. Experimental results show improved deformation reconstruction accuracy together with significantly reduced computation time, making the framework compatible with low-latency applications.

</details>


### [262] [On the disintegration of the stochastic majority vote: From PAC-Bayesian bounds to a self-bounding algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16803)
*Julien Bastian,Benjamin Leblanc,Pascal Germain,Amaury Habrard,Guillaume Metzler,Emilie Morvant,Paul Viallard*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对随机多数投票的去随机化框架，利用分解式PAC-Bayes理论直接在投票权重向量空间上将随机保证转化为单一确定性多数投票的证书，并推导出两类高概率泛化界，进而设计自限界学习算法优化确定性多数投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权多数投票是集成学习的核心，PAC-Bayes理论能为随机分类器提供紧的泛化保证，但分析确定性多数投票却依赖替代（surrogate）界，牺牲了紧致性。Zantedeschi等人提出的随机多数投票保证虽避免了替代界，却产生了随机化模型。本文旨在消除随机性，获得针对单一确定性多数投票的紧泛化保证。

Method: 作者提出去随机化框架，将最近发展的分解式（disintegrated）PAC-Bayes理论直接应用于多数投票的权重向量空间，从而把随机分类器的泛化保证转化为单个确定性多数投票的证书。据此推导出两类高概率泛化界，分别覆盖数据无关和数据相关的集成构造方式，并据此形成一种自我界定（self-bounding）的学习算法，直接优化确定性多数投票的保证。

Result: 得到了两类覆盖数据无关与数据相关集成构造的高概率泛化界，并提出了一种能直接优化确定性多数投票保证的自限界学习算法，将随机保证成功转化为确定性模型上的严格证书。

Conclusion: 该去随机化框架使得在不依赖替代界的情况下获得确定性多数投票的紧泛化保证成为可能，为集成学习在PAC-Bayes框架下提供了更实用的理论工具与算法。

Abstract: Weighted majority votes are central to many successful ensemble methods. PAC-Bayesian theory provides tight generalization guarantees for such models by analyzing the expected risk of stochastic classifiers, while analyzing the risk of deterministic majority votes relies on surrogate bounds. To avoid these surrogates, Zantedeschi et al. ( 2021) introduced guarantees for stochastic majority votes, but the resulting models remain randomized. In this paper, we propose a derandomization framework for stochastic majority votes. To do so, we apply recent advances in disintegrated PAC-Bayesian theory directly to the space of majority vote weight vectors, transforming stochastic guarantees into certificates for a single deterministic majority vote. We derive two families of high-probability generalization bounds, covering both data-independent and data-dependent constructions of the ensemble, which naturally lead to a self-bounding learning algorithm optimizing deterministic majority vote guarantees.

</details>


### [263] [Splitting the Difference: Interpretable Causal Forests for Treatment Effect Heterogeneity and Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16971)
*Nicolas Alexander Ihlo,Merle Behr*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策树/随机森林的个体处理效应（ITE）估计算法，它以不同的分裂准则运作，无需双重机器学习或正交化等额外技巧，即可同时实现预测与解释；在处理观测数据时可自动区分混杂因素与异质性来源，解释直接源于树结构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学、营销等领域，准确预测个体处理效应具有重要意义，但仅有可靠的预测不足以支持决策，还需理解为何某些个体的处理效应高于其他个体。现有方法（如广义随机森林）往往依赖双重机器学习或正交化等额外步骤，且缺乏直接从模型结构获得解释的能力，因此需要一种能同时兼顾预测准确性与可解释性的简单算法。

Method: 提出一种基于决策树和随机森林的ITE估计算法：其操作与标准随机森林相同，仅分裂准则不同。算法结合两种分裂准则——一种针对处理效应的异质性，另一种针对平均处理效应的偏差校正——从而改进分裂点选择，并自动区分混杂因素与导致异质性的特征。该算法无需单独估计完整倾向函数，也无需双重机器学习或正交化等处理。理论分析采用变点模型，假设潜在结果和处理倾向为阶梯函数。解释直接从拟合的树结构（树所使用的特征及其分裂统计量）获得，无需事后分析。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该简单算法在预测准确率上与现有方法相当、甚至更优，同时在可解释性上显著提升。

Conclusion: 该算法通过融合异质性分裂准则与偏差校正分裂准则，在不依赖双重机器学习或正交化的情况下，有效估计个体处理效应并实质性增强可解释性，兼具预测性能与理论支撑，为观测研究中的ITE估计与解读提供了一种简洁有力的方案。

Abstract: In various fields, such as medicine and marketing, accurately predicting individual treatment effects holds significant promise. However, achieving reliable predictions alone is often insufficient for making informed decisions; it is equally important to understand why the treatment effect is higher for some individuals than for others. To address this two-fold challenge of prediction and interpretation, we introduce an algorithm based on decision trees and random forests for estimating individual treatment effects. Our algorithm is simple: it operates exactly like a standard random forest, but with a different splitting criterion, and requires no additional workarounds such as double machine learning or orthogonalization as used in Generalized random forests. It handles observational studies with varying treatment propensities without requiring separate estimation of the full propensity function. This is achieved by combining two splitting criteria---one targeting heterogeneity in the treatment effect, the other targeting bias correction for the average treatment effect---which together improve split point selection and automatically distinguish confounders from features responsible for heterogeneity. As a result, interpretation follows directly from the fitted tree structure itself, that is, from which features the trees split on and with which split statistics, without requiring separate post-hoc analysis. For the theoretical analysis of this algorithm, we consider a change point model with step functions for potential outcomes and treatment propensity and provide insights into the theoretical underpinnings of our approach. Simulation studies show that our simple algorithm achieves comparable, and often better, prediction accuracy than existing methods, while substantially improving interpretability.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [Causal neural set filtering for online multi-target tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16054)
*Zhongdi Liu,Huangyu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果神经集合滤波（CNSF），一种仅编码当前量测、将历史证据保存在结构化递归轨迹状态中的神经集合滤波器，在仿真测试中相比 Track-MT3 显著降低 GOSPA/T-GOSPA，参数量更少、推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的多目标跟踪（如 MT3/Track-MT3）虽能联合学习数据关联与状态估计，但反复重编码量测窗口，产生大量冗余计算。作者希望设计一种避免重复编码、更高效的神经集合滤波方法。

Method: 提出 CNSF，只编码当前量测，将历史证据保存在结构化递归轨迹状态中。其核心机制包括：互斥 Sinkhorn 关联、基于关联条件的 Kalman 形更新（配合矩匹配）、以及带量测驱动出生的递归 Bernoulli 生命周期建模。这些机制分别施加软一对一约束、传播关联引起的状态不确定性，并在漏检与出生-消亡转换下支持存在性估计。

Result: 在留出的三机制仿真测试集上，CNSF 相比 Track-MT3 将平均 GOSPA 降低 19.3%、T-GOSPA 降低 30.4%，参数量减少 55.9%，单线程 CPU 推理速度提升 3.76 倍。

Conclusion: CNSF 在保持多目标跟踪性能的同时实现了更低的计算开销与更高的效率，验证了仅编码当前量测并借助递归状态承载历史证据这一设计的有效性。

Abstract: Transformer-based multi-target tracking (MTT) jointly learns data association and state estimation, but MT3/Track-MT3-style trackers repeatedly re-encode measurement windows, incurring redundant computation. We propose Causal Neural Set Filtering (CNSF)\footnote{\href{https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}{Code: https://github.com/daihuangyu/CNSF}}, a neural set filter that encodes only current measurements while carrying past evidence in a structured recursive track state. CNSF combines exclusive Sinkhorn association, association-conditioned Kalman-shaped updates with moment matching, and recurrent Bernoulli lifecycle modeling with measurement-driven birth. These mechanisms impose soft one-to-one constraints, propagate association-induced state uncertainty, and support existence estimation under missed detections and birth--death transitions. On a held-out three-regime simulated test set, CNSF reduces mean GOSPA and T-GOSPA relative to Track-MT3 by 19.3\% and 30.4\%, with 55.9\% fewer parameters and a $3.76\times$ speedup in single-thread CPU inference.

</details>


### [265] [Managing Action Preconditions in Neuro-Symbolic RL: Three Placement Strategies for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16056)
*Norbert Oswald,Fabian Deuser,Thomas Bräunl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将人类行为知识以"前置条件贝叶斯网络"形式注入强化学习智能体，针对"结构动作"（其合法性依赖前置条件）限制其触发时机，并在强化学习循环中测试了三种注入位置：符号验证器（仅推理时）、符号执行器（训练与推理全程）、符号学习者（将知识折入网络自身学习）。在两个基准（MiniGrid 长规划链、Fetch 连续操控）上，三种方案均优于强基线，尤其符号执行器在 MiniGrid 上以 98.2% 的解质量超越 PPO+RND 基线的 88.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会把熟悉情境中的行为知识带入新任务，而非从头重学。强化学习智能体也应如此，已知行为模式不必重学，只需加以应用。神经符号强化学习通过注入符号知识来桥接先验知识与强化学习，但知识注入的位置至关重要：位置选择不当会产生"幻觉前置条件"，在动态变化环境中表现为安全与可靠性问题。

Method: 将行为知识形式化为结构动作（如拾取钥匙、抓取方块、开关门、投放物体等合法性依赖前置条件的动作）之上的前置条件贝叶斯网络。该网络限制这些动作何时可触发，并将其以三种位置注入强化学习循环：(1) 符号验证器——仅在推理时查询，前置条件成立才触发结构动作；(2) 符号执行器——训练与推理全程生效，在学习过程中管理结构动作的使用；(3) 符号学习者——把知识折入网络，由网络自己学习结构动作的限制与使用。

Result: 在两个相反情境的基准上测试：一个基于长而有序的规划链（MiniGrid），一个基于连续操控（Fetch）。在解质量、样本效率与可追溯性上与强基线对比，收益显著。在 MiniGrid 上三种注入位置均提升解质量，符号执行器以 98.2% 领先，高于 PPO+RND 基线的 88.8%。

Conclusion: 将前置条件贝叶斯网络形式化的行为知识注入强化学习，能显著提升智能体的解质量、样本效率与可追溯性。知识注入的位置是关键设计选择，不同位置（验证器、执行器、学习者）带来不同收益，其中符号执行器在 MiniGrid 上表现最优。摘要中 Fetch 部分未完整给出。

Abstract: Humans carry behaviour knowledge of how to act in familiar situations into every new task rather than relearning it from scratch. There is no reason a Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent shouldn't do the same: known behaviour patterns need not be learned, only applied. Neuro-symbolic RL bridges prior knowledge and RL by injecting symbolic knowledge alongside a learned policy. The point at which this knowledge is integrated is critical: a poor choice can produce, for instance, hallucinated preconditions, which surface as safety and reliability problems in agents acting in changing environments. We formalise this behavioural knowledge as a precondition Bayesian network (BN) over the agent's \emph{structural actions} - the actions whose legality depends on preconditions, such as picking up a key, grasping a block, toggling a door, or dropping an object. The BN restricts when these actions may fire, and we inject it into the RL loop at three placements: (1) a \emph{symbolic verifier}, consulted only at inference, that fires a structural action once its preconditions hold; (2) a \emph{symbolic enforcer}, active during both training and inference, that governs structural-action use throughout learning; and (3) a \emph{symbolic learner}, which folds the knowledge into the network and learns the restriction and use of structural actions itself. To test the three variants we run experiments on two benchmarks with opposite regimes: one built on long, ordered planning chains, the other on continuous manipulation. We compare against strong baselines on solution quality, sample efficiency, and traceability. The payoff is substantial. On MiniGrid, all three placements improve the \emph{solution quality} over the PPO+RND baseline, the symbolic enforcer leading at $98.2\%$ against the baseline's $88.8\%$. On Fetch, $\dots$

</details>


### [266] [OmniHarness: Harnessing Generalizable Visual Generation via Symbolic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16057)
*Xu Xu,Jinxiu Liu,Zhangbo Qiao,Jiaxing Lu,Xiangyu Zhang,Yubin Gu,Fangwei Ning,Yan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniHarness 提出一种基于符号策略学习的通用视觉生成框架，通过将经验抽象为可实例化、适配与组合的符号策略，并以中间验证引导执行与失败恢复，在多个基准上显著提升视觉生成表现（ComfyBench Creative 任务解决率达 95.0%，超越最强基线 27.5 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态大模型与多智能体系统在视觉生成中存在三个局限：(1) 方法常蒸馏任务特定经验，泛化性有限；(2) 反思往往推迟到任务完成后才进行；(3) 知识仅在响应下游任务需求时才被动获取。作者旨在解决这三点，实现更具普适性和持续进化能力的视觉生成。

Method: 提出 OmniHarness 框架，核心为符号策略学习。它将已验证的执行过程抽象为面向视觉生成任务族的符号策略，保留共享流程与适用条件、去除实例特定输入；对新任务通过实例化、适配、组合这些策略来执行；执行过程中由中间验证引导细化与失败恢复；并通过自导探究在明确下游目标前自主生成并执行接近能力边界的练习任务，利用执行反馈持续更新策略，全程保持模型参数固定不变。

Result: 在 6 个基准、3 个 MLLM 骨干和 3 个视觉智能体框架上均表现优异并实现持续能力扩展。在 ComfyBench 的 Creative 任务上达到 95.0% 的解决率，比最强基线高出 27.5 个百分点；冻结的策略快照还能通过即插即用方式提升现有视觉智能体系统。

Conclusion: OmniHarness 通过将经验转化为可复用的符号策略、即时中间验证和自导探究，克服了任务特定性、延迟反思与被动知识获取三大局限，在不更新模型参数的条件下实现通用化视觉生成与能力持续增强，并具备良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and multi-agent systems have advanced visual generation. However, three limitations remain. (1) Existing methods often distill task-specific experience with limited generalizability. (2) Reflection is often deferred until task completion. (3) Knowledge is often acquired only in response to downstream task demands. To address these limitations, we introduce OmniHarness, a framework for generalizable visual generation via symbolic policy learning. OmniHarness abstracts verified executions into symbolic policies for visual generation task families, capturing shared procedures and applicability conditions while removing instance-specific inputs. The harness instantiates, adapts, and composes these policies for new tasks. Intermediate verification guides refinement and failure recovery during execution. Through self-directed inquiry, OmniHarness autonomously generates and executes practice tasks near its capability limits before downstream objectives are specified. Execution feedback continually refines the policies while model parameters remain fixed. Experiments across six benchmarks, three MLLM backbones, and three visual agent frameworks demonstrate strong performance and continual capability expansion. On ComfyBench's Creative tasks, OmniHarness achieves a 95.0% resolve rate, exceeding the strongest baseline by 27.5 percentage points. Frozen policy snapshots improve existing visual agent systems through plug-and-play reuse.

</details>


### [267] [Driver Behavior Estimation at Signalized Intersections Using a Physics-Constrained Decision-Conditioned Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16058)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,Pavel Laskov,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文收集真实路口数据，分析并预测人类驾驶员在信号灯过渡（黄灯）期间的通停决策与纵向轨迹行为，提出"决策预测+决策条件轨迹生成"的两阶段框架，实现高精度、类人且考虑舒适度的刹车行为建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号交叉口的闯红灯与急刹车是交通事故的重要诱因。现有方法缺乏对小样本人类驾驶行为（尤其考虑心理/生理状态）的精细建模，难以同时预测驾驶员"停还是走"的决策与随后的纵向加速度轨迹及舒适度。

Method: 采集449组不同速度与距离条件下的真实接近路口数据，使用RTK级GNSS厘米级定位记录车辆运动，同步监测驾驶人心率与多级舒适度评分，并做时空标定对齐车辆状态与信号配时。统计分析表明所需减速度是通停决策的主导预测因子，并对峰值减速度做异方差高斯建模得到五个经验舒适区间。据此提出两阶段框架：第一阶段预测二元机动决策；第二阶段用决策条件的自回归Transformer生成纵向加速度轨迹，并施加目标状态条件与冲击度（jerk）限制等物理约束。

Result: 所提架构优于基线方法，加速度MAE为0.49 m/s²，距离MAE为0.62 m；并能从黄灯起始的单帧快照估计驾驶员未来的停车舒适度等级。定性结果显示生成的刹车行为具有真实人类特征。

Conclusion: 通过真实数据与两阶段决策-轨迹建模框架，实现了对驾驶员通停决策、纵向轨迹及停车舒适度的准确预测，体现了物理约束与数据驱动结合在类人驾驶行为建模中的有效性，数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Red-light violations and harsh braking at signalized intersections are major contributors to traffic accidents. This paper analyzes and predicts human driver decision-making and longitudinal trajectory behavior during traffic light signal transitions. We collected a diverse real-world dataset comprising 449 approach runs under varying speed and distance conditions. Vehicle motion was recorded using RTK-corrected GNSS with centimeter-level accuracy, and driver heart rate and multi-level comfort ratings were monitored. Spatial and temporal calibration ensured precise alignment between vehicle state and signal timing. Statistical analysis identifies required deceleration as the dominant single predictor of the stop-go decision, and heteroscedastic Gaussian modeling of peak deceleration reveals five empirical comfort ranges derived from human stopping behavior. Based on this insight, we propose a two-stage modeling framework. Stage 1 predicts the binary maneuver decision, and Stage 2 generates the longitudinal acceleration trajectory using a decision-conditioned autoregressive Transformer with physics constraints, including target-state conditioning and jerk limits. The proposed architecture outperforms baseline methods and achieves 0.49m/s^2 acceleration MAE and 0.62m distance MAE. It also estimates the future stopping-comfort level of the human driver from a single yellow-onset snapshot. Qualitative results demonstrate realistic human-like braking behavior. The dataset and source code are publicly available.

</details>


### [268] [HintMiner: Automatic Question Hints Mining From Q&A Web Posts with Language Model via Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16060)
*Zhenyu Zhang,JiuDong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 HintMiner，一个从在线问答帖子中自动生成问题提示（hints）的工具，采用自监督训练的神经编码器-解码器（MiningNet），在 Stack Overflow 上取得较好 BLEU/ROUGE 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户常需要在线提问寻求答案，但像 Stack Overflow 这样的问答论坛往往无法及时、恰当地回应用户问题，因此需要一种自动生成问题提示的工具来帮助用户找到答案。

Method: 提出 HintMiner，利用机器阅读理解与序列生成技术自动为用户问题生成提示。流程为：先检索大量网络问答帖子，再利用基于语言模型构建的 MiningNet 从帖子中抽取提示；MiningNet 是基于 Transformer 与复制机制的神经编码器-解码器模型，并利用海量在线问答帖子设计自监督目标进行训练。

Result: 在 60,000 个 Stack Overflow 问题上评估，实验表明方法有效：平均 BLEU 分数达 36.17%，平均 ROUGE-2 分数达 36.29%；工具与实验数据均已公开。

Conclusion: HintMiner 能够有效、自动地为用户问题生成提示，缓解问答论坛响应不及时的问题，且代码与数据开源可复现。

Abstract: Users often need ask questions and seek answers online. The Question - Answering (QA) forums such as Stack Overflow cannot always respond to the questions timely and properly. In this paper, we propose HintMiner, a novel automatic question hints mining tool for users to help them find answers. HintMiner leverages the machine comprehension and sequence generation techniques to automatically generate hints for users' questions. It firstly retrieve many web Q\&A posts and then extract some hints from the posts using MiningNet that is built via a language model. Using the huge amount of online Q\&A posts, we design a self-supervised objective to train the MiningNet that is a neural encoder-decoder model based on the transformer and copying mechanisms. We have evaluated HintMiner on 60,000 Stack Overflow questions. The experiment results show that the proposed approach is effective. For example, HintMiner achieves an average BLEU score of 36.17\% and an average ROUGE-2 score of 36.29\%. Our tool and experimental data are publicly available.

</details>


### [269] [POSPAN: Position-Constrained Span Masking for Language Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16061)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Lei Shen,Yuming Zhao,Meng Chen,Xiaodong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 POSPAN 框架，将位置约束分布与跨度长度分布相结合来设计多样的跨度遮蔽策略，统一了所有现有跨度级 MLM 方法，并在 NLU 基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨度级掩码语言模型只考虑跨度长度分布，却忽略跨度之间的依赖关系，假设被遮蔽跨度的位置服从均匀分布，未能充分利用实体/短语及其依赖对语言理解的重要性。

Method: 提出 POSPAN 通用框架，通过组合跨度长度分布与位置约束分布，实现多样的位置受限跨度遮蔽策略，并统一所有现有跨度级遮蔽方法；同时从理论上对遮蔽语言模型中的位置约束进行分析。

Result: 在多个 NLU 基准数据集上的实验表明，位置约束能广泛增强跨度级遮蔽，最优 POSPAN 设置一致优于仅使用跨度长度的方法和原始单 token MLM。

Conclusion: 位置约束能有效增强跨度级遮蔽，POSPAN 的优越表现及其理论分析证明了位置约束的合理性与必要性。

Abstract: Span-level masked language modeling (MLM) has shown to be advantageous to pre-trained language models over the original single-token MLM, as entities/phrases and their dependencies are critical to language understanding. Previous works only consider span length with some discrete distributions, while the dependencies among spans are ignored, i.e., assuming that the positions of masked spans are uniformly distributed. In this paper, we present POSPAN, a general framework to allow diverse position-constrained span masking strategies via the combination of span length distribution and position constraint distribution, which unifies all existing span-level masking methods. To verify the effectiveness of POSPAN in pre-training, we evaluate it on the datasets from several NLU benchmarks. Experimental results indicate that the position constraint is capable of enhancing span-level masking broadly, and our best POSPAN setting consistently outperforms its span-length-only counterparts and vanilla MLM. We also conduct theoretical analysis for the position constraint in masked language models to shed light on the reason why POSPAN works well, demonstrating the rationality and necessity of POSPAN.

</details>


### [270] [Signed p-adic Residual Encodings of Finite-Domain All-Different Systems with a Sudoku Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16063)
*Greg Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带符号加权的仿射 p-adic 残差目标函数，将其作为有限域约束的原生编码形式；证明了当素数将有限字母表分离时，可通过正一元行"钉住"各系数的取值集合、负行奖励不等端点或子句满足，并给出坐标支配定理保证全局极小点落在有限域内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将有限域约束（如 all-different、CNF 可满足性）直接编码为带符号加权的仿射 p-adic 残差目标，从而避免传统 one-hot 提升等间接表示，实现更紧凑、更"原生"的约束表示与优化。

Method: 构造带符号加权仿射 p-adic 残差目标：对于能够分离有限字母表的素数，使用充分加权的正一元行将每个系数"钉"到其允许集合，负行则奖励端点不相等或子句满足；并建立坐标支配（coordinatewise domination）定理，证明每个全局极小点都落在有限域内。

Result: 在有限域上，该损失函数在加性常数意义下等价于 all-different 冲突计数或已满足 CNF 子句数的相反数；标准数独可作为 81 个系数、无需 one-hot 提升的案例研究；客户端实现暴露了生成的数据帧、算术运算、诊断与搜索过程。

Conclusion: 带符号加权仿射 p-adic 残差目标能够原生编码有限域约束，坐标支配定理保证全局极小点位于有限域内，且损失退化为经典组合目标；该方法在标准数独等场景可直接应用，无需 one-hot 提升。

Abstract: We study signed, weighted affine $p$-adic residual objectives as native encodings of finite-domain constraints. For primes that separate the finite alphabet, sufficiently weighted positive unary rows pin each coefficient to its allowed set, while negative rows reward unequal endpoints or clause satisfaction. A coordinatewise domination theorem places every global minimiser in the finite domain; there the loss is, up to an additive constant, the all-different conflict count or the negative number of satisfied CNF clauses. Standard Sudoku provides an $81$-coefficient case study without a one-hot lift. A client-side implementation exposes the generated dataframes, arithmetic, diagnostics, and searches.

</details>


### [271] [You Don't Need To Train: Agentic Heuristic Learning Studio for Executable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16065)
*Siyu Yuan,He Zhang,Sizhen Bian,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AHL Studio 工具，将人类活动识别（HAR）从基于梯度的神经网络训练转向受人类认知学习启发的代理式启发式学习（AHL），在十一个数据集上实现了可解释、可编辑、可复现且无需 LLM 的边缘端可执行策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 HAR 依赖于神经网络的梯度式训练，缺乏可解释性与可编辑性。作者受人类认知学习方式（记忆示例、形成规则、修复错误）启发，希望探索一种不依赖反向传播、无需部署 LLM 即可在边缘端运行的可检查、可编辑、可回放的替代学习范式。

Method: 提出 Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) 框架：由学习时智能体对传感器协议进行推理，提出可执行的启发式策略，记录修复轨迹，并导出一个不依赖 LLM 的策略用于边缘部署。AHL Studio 是该框架的实现工具，提供从数据集观测到边缘导向导出的端到端工作流，聚焦于 HAR 基准数据集族。

Result: 在已评估的十一个 HAR 数据集上，AHL 策略达到了较强的可执行策略性能，同时保持可检查（inspectable）、可编辑（editable）与可回放（replayable）的特性。

Conclusion: AHL Studio 验证了以启发式策略替代梯度训练进行 HAR 的可行性，展示了兼具性能与可解释性的边缘端部署方案，为 HAR 提供了一条受认知学习启发的补充路径。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) is usually framed as gradient-based training of neural networks. Agentic Heuristic Learning (AHL) Studio explores a complementary view inspired by human cognitive learning: people learn activities by remembering examples, forming rules, and repairing mistakes, not by backpropagating. This proposed tool implements AHL for HAR: a learning-time agent reasons over sensor protocols, proposes executable heuristic policies, records repair traces, and exports an LLM-free policy for edge deployment. We focus on the HAR benchmark family and provide an end-to-end workflow from dataset observation to edge-oriented export. On eleven HAR datasets evaluated so far, AHL policies reach strong executable-policy performance while remaining inspectable, editable, and replayable \footnote{https://github.com/zhaxidele/ahl-ts-studio}.

</details>


### [272] [Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16170)
*Jordan Eckert,Henry Schenck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPINE方法，用嵌入的1-复形（骨架原型）替代传统点集来进行原型缩减，在17个数据集上取得最高平均准确率和最佳平均排名，且在统计检验上显著优于多数对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原型缩减方法返回有限点集，忽略了数据内在的几何与拓扑结构；作者希望用更丰富的1-复形结构（由局部聚类连接而成的图）来表示每一类，使决策规则能够利用图的分段信息，从而提升原型缩减的效果。

Method: SPINE为每个类别构建一个嵌入的1-复形，其初始边集由类条件Mapper图决定（由数据决定哪些局部聚类被连接）；后续阶段在分类目标下拟合顶点；分类时将一个观测点分配给距离其最近的那个类别的复形，使得图的分段直接进入决策规则。

Result: 在17个基准数据集上采用分层10折交叉验证，并与7种原型缩减方法在匹配预算下比较：SPINE取得最高平均准确率和最佳平均排名；在带Holm校正的Wilcoxon符号秩检验下，显著优于7个竞争对手中的5个。预算扫描显示，当原型稀缺时，使用完整图分段的决策规则贡献最大；整体方法在中等预算下最具竞争力。构建成本上SPINE与判别式方法相当，且在17个数据集中的14个上快于广义学习向量量化。

Conclusion: SPINE通过用嵌入1-复形代替点集来表示原型，将图分段纳入决策规则，在准确率、排名和统计显著性上均优于多数现有原型缩减方法，并在计算成本上具有竞争力。

Abstract: Prototype reduction replaces a training set with a smaller representation, and the established methods return a finite set of points. We propose Skeletal Prototypes on Iterative Nerve Expansions (SPINE). The model for each class is an embedded 1-complex rather than a point set. Its initial edge set is a class-conditional Mapper graph, so the data decide which localized clusters are joined. Later phases fit the vertices under a classification objective, and an observation is assigned to the class whose complex is nearest. The segments therefore enter the decision rule and not only the fitting. We evaluate SPINE on seventeen benchmark datasets under stratified 10-fold cross validation, against seven other prototype reduction methods at a matched budget. SPINE attains the highest mean accuracy and the best average rank. It is significantly better than five of the seven competitors under Wilcoxon signed-rank tests with Holm correction. A budget sweep shows that the decision rule using the entire graph segments contribute most when prototypes are scarce, while the method as a whole competes best at moderate budgets. Construction cost places SPINE with the discriminative methods, and it is faster than generalized learning vector quantization on fourteen of the seventeen datasets.

</details>


### [273] [A panoramic aerodynamic performance prediction method for turbomachinery cascades using transformer-enhanced neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16066)
*Qineng Wang,Zhendong Guo,Liming Song,Tianyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全景性能预测框架，先预测纳维-斯托克斯方程的基本物理量（温度、压力、密度），再据此预测涡轮级子午面的关键性能参数，并引入变压器增强神经算子（TNO）提升精度，实现类CFD模拟器功能，计算成本降低四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶轮机械设计中需要灵活、快速的气动性能评估，而传统CFD模拟计算成本高昂；现有预测模型大多直接预测目标函数，缺乏通用性，难以像CFD模拟器那样灵活预测设计者关心的各种目标。

Method: 提出全景性能预测框架：不直接预测目标函数，而是先预测纳维-斯托克斯方程的基本参数（温度、压力、密度等基本物理量），再基于这些量预测涡轮级子午面的关键性能参数。框架内引入变压器增强神经算子（TNO）以提升预测精度，并以Rotor 37叶片为参考，训练TNO预测跨声速压气机叶片子午面性能。

Result: TNO能准确预测等熵效率、质量流量、总压比分布等总量参数，其预测误差小于当前最先进的深度学习算子（如FNO和DeepONet）。TNO应用于敏感性分析和多目标函数优化等下游任务时，表现几乎等同于CFD模拟器，且将下游任务计算成本降低四个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提TNO在解决各类下游任务中的有效性和可靠性得到充分验证，证明其可近乎替代CFD模拟器，同时大幅降低计算成本，为叶轮机械设计的快速气动性能评估提供了可行的全景预测方案。

Abstract: To enable flexible and rapid aerodynamic performance evaluation in turbomachinery design, this paper proposes a panoramic performance prediction framework. Unlike most previous prediction models that directly predict the objective functions of interest, our approach first predicts the basic parameters of the Navier-Stokes equations, such as temperature, pressure, and density. Utilizing these basic physical quantities, it subsequently predicts key performance parameters of the turbine stage meridian plane.
