We further develop a reward-compatible bounded mixing (RBM) mechanism for $γ\mathrm{OPD}$ that balances verifiable outcome feedback with the discounted OPD advantage to move beyond purely teacher-dependent optimization. Experiments on mathematical and code reasoning demonstrate consistent improvements over existing OPD methods across vanilla, size-mismatched, and multi-teacher distillation settings.

</details>


### [340] [SemanticAdv: Generating Adversarial Examples via Attribute-conditional Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/1906.07927)
*Haonan Qiu,Chaowei Xiao,Lei Yang,Xinchen Yan,Honglak Lee,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemanticAdv 算法，通过操控图像的语义属性而非限制 Lp 范数来生成"无限制对抗样本"，可有效欺骗人脸验证、关键点检测及现实黑盒服务（如 Azure），并推广至街景图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗样本大多通过限制扰动 Lp 范数来保证"细微扰动"，但忽略了语义层面的操纵。作者希望探索语义操纵对 DNN 预测的影响，生成不受 Lp 约束的"无限制对抗样本"。

Method: 提出 SemanticAdv 算法，利用解耦的语义因子，通过改变受控的语义属性生成对抗扰动，使模型朝着各种"对抗性"目标方向被欺骗。

Result: 实验表明，基于语义的对抗样本不仅能欺骗人脸验证和关键点检测等不同学习任务，还能借助可迁移性对 Azure 人脸验证服务等真实世界黑盒系统取得高定向攻击成功率；在街景图像上生成语义扰动进一步验证了其适用范围。

Conclusion: 受控语义操纵生成的对抗样本有助于加深对 DNN 脆弱性的理解，并为潜在防御方法提供启示。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have achieved great success in various applications due to their strong expressive power. However, recent studies have shown that DNNs are vulnerable to adversarial examples which are manipulated instances targeting to mislead DNNs to make incorrect predictions. Currently, most such adversarial examples try to guarantee "subtle perturbation" by limiting the $L_p$ norm of the perturbation. In this paper, we aim to explore the impact of semantic manipulation on DNNs predictions by manipulating the semantic attributes of images and generate "unrestricted adversarial examples".
  In particular, we propose an algorithm \emph{SemanticAdv} which leverages disentangled semantic factors to generate adversarial perturbation by altering controlled semantic attributes to fool the learner towards various "adversarial" targets. We conduct extensive experiments to show that the semantic based adversarial examples can not only fool different learning tasks such as face verification and landmark detection, but also achieve high targeted attack success rate against \emph{real-world black-box} services such as Azure face verification service based on transferability.
  To further demonstrate the applicability of \emph{SemanticAdv} beyond face recognition domain, we also generate semantic perturbations on street-view images. Such adversarial examples with controlled semantic manipulation can shed light on further understanding about vulnerabilities of DNNs as well as potential defensive approaches.

</details>


### [341] [Structural Negative Transfer in Federated Graph Neural Networks: Diagnosis, Causal Investigation, and the Limits of Divergence-Aware Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16977)
*Chethana Prasad Kabgere,Shylaja SS*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦图神经网络中的"结构性负迁移"——当客户端图在拓扑结构上存在根本差异时，共享权重在不同图上运行所导致的性能损失。作者发现结构异常客户端仅因参与联邦就损失了一半以上可达精度，但经过扩展实验与因果干预后，初始信号未能稳健复现，仅存一个适度的、与度数相关的部分可复现信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有非IID联邦学习研究表明联邦平均能承受标签与特征分布的差异，但作者质疑这一假设是否能承受图神经网络特有的另一种差异——客户端图在结构（拓扑）本身上的差异，即共享权重需在根本不同的拓扑上运行。

Method: 在真实引文网络与合成结构代理组成的联邦中，作者：(1) 度量结构异常客户端参与联邦的性能损失；(2) 用留一法计算训练前的两个无标签结构统计量（度散度、谱散度）并检验其与损失的关联；(3) 将客户端规模从六个扩展到二十个，并移除领域对比以测试稳健性；(4) 进行隔离拓扑的因果干预；(5) 测试度数归一化机制（24个种子）；(6) 对五种候选修复方案与调优基线及结构盲匹配对照进行比较。

Result: 结构异常客户端因加入联邦损失了超过一半可达精度；六客户端联邦中两个无标签结构统计量与损失强相关；扩展至二十客户端后度数散度关联减弱但仍存在且不受领域对比影响，谱散度未复现（归因于留一参考池组成的混淆）；拓扑因果干预无显著效应；度数归一化在二十四种子下成立但修正后无法解释损失；最佳候选修复仅在有结构盲匹配对照前超过调优基线，对照后增益消失。

Conclusion: 结构性负迁移在初始小规模实验中表现明显，但无法在大规模下稳健复制。最终存留的只是一个适度的、部分可复现的度数特定信号，尚不能作为经得起规模检验的预测因子。

Abstract: Federated learning lets multiple participants train a shared model without pooling raw data, by exchanging locally trained model updates instead. Federated averaging assumes that averaging local models is a reasonable way to solve one shared problem when participants' data are broadly similar. Work on non-IID federated learning has shown that this assumption can withstand differences in label and feature distributions. We ask whether it survives a different strain specific to graph neural networks, where client graphs differ not in label or feature distribution but in structure itself, requiring the same shared weights to operate over fundamentally different topologies. We call the resulting harm structural negative transfer. In a federation of real citation networks and synthetic structural proxies, a structurally atypical client lost more than half its achievable accuracy simply by joining. In an initial six-client federation, two label-free structural statistics computable before training were strongly associated with this harm. Expanding to twenty clients showed that degree divergence remained associated with harm, although more weakly, and survived removal of domain contrast. Spectral divergence did not replicate, which we trace to a confound caused by the composition of the reference pool used for leave-one-out statistics. A causal intervention isolating topology found no significant effect. A degree-normalization mechanism held across twenty-four seeds but did not explain the harm when corrected. The best of five candidate fixes beat a tuned baseline only until a matched, structurally blind control was applied, after which the gain disappeared. What survives is a modest, partially replicated, degree-specific signal that is not yet a validated predictor at scale.

</details>


### [342] [CLARE: Scalable Class-Incremental Continual Learning via a Sparsity-Based Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17026)
*Yunxiang Fu,Meng Lou,Zicheng Liao,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLARE，一种基于稀疏化的持续学习框架，通过“先识别任务关键稀疏掩码、再掩码约束微调”的两阶段稀疏适配器机制，在共享适配器空间中累积全部任务，缓解灾难性遗忘与可塑性损失，并在长任务序列基准 Omnibenchmark-1k 上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需要在学习新知识与保留旧知识之间取得平衡。尽管利用预训练模型已大幅推进持续学习，但现有方法在顺序学习众多任务时存在可扩展性瓶颈：由于任务间干扰和可塑性损失，性能会随任务数增加而退化。受“稀疏微调能达到与全量微调相当性能”这一证据启发，作者希望利用稀疏性来缓解任务间干扰、提升长任务序列下的可扩展性。

Method: 提出稀疏驱动的持续学习框架 CLARE，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段通过稀疏诱导目标识别出一个稀疏的、任务关键的参数掩码；第二阶段执行掩码约束微调，仅优化掩码选中的参数。该两阶段稀疏适配器机制使所有任务累积到共享的适配器空间中，同时减少跨任务的破坏性干扰。

Result: 在长任务序列基准 Omnibenchmark-1k 上，CLARE 在最终精度方面大幅超越强基线，例如在学习 100 个任务后，分别比 EASE 提升 4.64% 和 13.34%。

Conclusion: CLARE 通过稀疏适配器机制有效提升持续学习的可扩展性，在长任务序列场景下显著降低任务间干扰与可塑性损失，取得优于现有强基线的最终精度。

Abstract: Continual learning must balance the learning of new knowledge with the retention of previously learned knowledge to incrementally learn tasks from a data stream without catastrophic forgetting. While leveraging pretrained models has significantly advanced continual learning, existing methods exhibit a scalability bottleneck when trained sequentially on many tasks, suffering from performance degradation due to inter-task interference and loss of plasticity. Inspired by evidence that sparse fine-tuning achieves performance comparable to full fine-tuning, this paper presents a novel sparsity-driven continual learning framework. Our continual learning method, termed CLARE, operates in two stages: it first identifies a sparse, task-critical parameter mask via a sparsity-inducing objective, then performs mask-constrained fine-tuning by only optimizing parameters selected by the mask. This two-stage sparse adapter mechanism enables all tasks to be accumulated within a shared adapter space while reducing destructive interference across tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate the scalability of CLARE. On the long task-sequence benchmark Omnibenchmark-1k, CLARE outperforms strong baselines in final accuracy by a large margin, e.g, improving EASE by 4.64% and 13.34% after learning 100 tasks, respectively.

</details>


### [343] [Distributed JEPA: A Self-Supervised Framework for Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17029)
*Liana Toderean,Tudor Cioara,Vasilis Michalakopoulos,Efstathios Sarantinopoulos,Ionut Anghel,Elissaios Sarmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），用于对异构能源时间序列进行自监督学习，在缺失数据场景下展现出与Transformer相当甚至更优的预测性能，且表示更稳定、鲁棒性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能源预测方案依赖任务特定监督和特定能源资产表示，限制了可迁移性，也难以捕捉异构资产间通用的时序动态规律。因此需要一种无需标签、能跨异构资产学习通用时序表示的自监督方法。

Method: 提出分布式联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）：在共享嵌入空间中整合时序观测与上下文信息，通过预测被掩蔽时序片段的潜在表示进行学习；为防止表示坍缩，训练结合潜在空间预测目标、协方差正则化与时序方差正则化。

Result: 在能源消费与发电数据集上、数据退化场景下评估，并与Transformer基线对比。学到的表示保持稳定（余弦相似度≈0.98，有效秩185-235）。JEPA在建筑能耗数据上与Transformer性能相当；在5个消费者集群中3个取得更高R²；在10个未见光伏中9个超越基线（R²=0.73-0.88 vs <0.45），且对缺失数据更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 分布式JEPA能够以自监督方式从异构能源时序中学习稳定、可迁移的通用表示，在未见资产和缺失数据场景下表现优于或媲美监督式Transformer基线，展现出更强的泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Traditional energy forecasting solutions rely on task-specific supervision and energy asset representations, limiting transferability and the ability to capture general temporal dynamics across heterogeneous assets. We address this by proposing a distributed Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) for self-supervised learning from heterogeneous energy time-series. The framework predicts latent representations of masked temporal segments while integrating temporal observations and contextual information within a shared embedding space. To prevent representation collapse, training combines a latent-space predictive objective with covariance and temporal variance regularization. The evaluation was conducted on energy consumption and generation datasets under data-degradation scenarios and compared with a Transformer forecasting baseline. The learned representations remained stable (cosine similarity $\approx 0.98$; effective rank 185-235). JEPA achieved performance comparable to a Transformer on building energy data, higher $R^2$ in 3/5 consumer clusters, and outperformed the baseline on 9/10 unseen PVs ($R^2$=0.73-0.88 vs. <0.45), while showing greater robustness to missing data.

</details>


### [344] [Learning Options for Compositional Motor Control with Adapter Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17042)
*Sreejan Kumar,Marcelo Mattar,Lea Duncker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种共享循环核心+残差适配器（低秩扰动）的新架构，用离散潜码选择适配器来端到端学习运动基元，并在闭环仿生控制中实现对新动作序列的泛化，泛化误差比任务输入条件的多任务基线降低多达一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期神经科学理论提出运动基元可被实现为共享循环网络上的低秩扰动，但尚未解决此类系统如何学习的问题。作者希望将这一原理转化为可端到端学习的架构，从而模仿熟练运动控制的灵活基元组合能力。

Method: 设计一个共享循环核心网络，其上挂载一组残差适配器，每个适配器由一个离散潜码选择；在闭环仿生控制任务中端到端训练。训练后，适配器在没有显式秩约束的情况下自发形成对循环动态的低秩扰动，使各任务表征落在共享核心网络的不同子空间。随后在冻结整个网络的前提下，优化一个简单的高层策略来序列化地组合这些低秩适配器，以生成新的分布外运动。

Result: 适配器涌现出对循环动态的低秩扰动，且任务表征分布于共享核心网络的互异子空间；高层策略能将这些低秩适配器序列化组合，在闭环控制设定下生成新颖的、分布外的运动序列。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了“运动基元=共享循环网络上的低秩扰动”这一神经科学原理可用于端到端技能学习，并展现出对新运动序列的强泛化能力，泛化误差比任务输入条件的多任务基线最高降低一个数量级。

Abstract: Learning flexible motor primitives is a hallmark of skilled motor control. Recent neuroscience theory proposes that motor primitives may be implemented as low-rank perturbations of a shared recurrent network, but leaves open how such a system is learned. We translate this principle into a novel architecture for learning motor skills end-to-end: a shared recurrent core modulated by a bank of residual adapters, each selected by a discrete latent code. Trained on closed-loop biomechanical control, the adapters develop emergent low-rank perturbations of the recurrent dynamics despite no architectural rank constraint, placing task representations in disparate subspaces of the shared core network. A simple high-level policy over the learned options, optimized while the whole network is frozen, sequences the low-rank adapters to produce novel out-of-distribution movements. We demonstrate the ability to generalize to novel motor sequences within the closed-loop control setting, improving on the generalization error of a task-input-conditioned multitask baseline by upto order of magnitude.

</details>


### [345] [Repurposing Unified Topological Signatures for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17061)
*Sanyam Sanjay Jain,Anshika Krishnatray,Aditya Sharma,Vinti Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将统一拓扑签名（UTS）集成到消息传递图神经网络（GNN）的训练中，通过三种架构干预手段（数据增强、正则化、池化）突破1-WL同构测试的表达力上限，并在多个图分类基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递GNN的判别能力受限于Weisfeiler-Lehman（1-WL）图同构测试，无法区分局部邻域结构相同但全局拓扑不同的非图同构图。UTS虽能从持续同调中捕捉1-WL GNN无法获取的多尺度全局拓扑结构，但此前仅被用于事后嵌入空间分析，尚未被用于改进GNN训练本身。

Method: 作者引入两种互补的UTS签名：Graph_UTS（输入图拓扑的静态签名）和Embedding_UTS（演化嵌入拓扑的动态签名）。通过三种架构干预将其集成到GNN训练：(i) UTS-Aug：将编码真实拓扑的UTS读出特征与标准readout拼接作数据增强；(ii) UTS-Reg：以拓扑正则项约束表示坍缩；(iii) UTS-Pool：以拓扑为指导的池化，保留结构关键节点。此外，利用UTS作为逐层诊断工具量化训练过程中的过平滑。理论上证明将UTS纳入GNN优化能严格扩展GNN表达力至1-WL层级之上。

Result: 在三个图分类基准上，Graph-UTS、Dual-UTS和UTS-Pool在所有数据集上均提升准确率；Embedding-UTS提升幅度较小但同样一致；UTS-Reg的收益因图域而异。Graph-UTS增强最高提升5.8%的准确率，UTS-Reg最高提升1.9%，UTS-Pool达到与TOGL相当的性能。

Conclusion: 将UTS集成到GNN训练中能够严格扩展GNN的表达力，使其超越1-WL层级，并在多个图分类任务上带来一致且可观的性能提升，证实了拓扑签名作为GNN架构组件的实用价值。

Abstract: Message-passing Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) iteratively propagate and aggregate local neighborhood information followed by global readout to learn graph representations. However, their discriminative power is upper-bounded by the Weisfeiler--Lehman (1-WL) graph isomorphism test. This prevents GNNs from distinguishing certain non-isomorphic graphs with identical local neighborhood structures, often leading to similar graph representations. Unified Topological Signatures (UTS) capture compact, multi-scale representation of global graph topology derived from persistent homology. We introduce two complementary UTS signatures: Graph_UTS- a static signature of the input graph topology, and Embedding_UTS- a dynamic signature of the evolving embedding topology. They encode structural information inaccessible to 1-WL-based message-passing GNNs, yet their capabilities are explored solely for post-hoc embedding-space analysis. We integrate UTS into GNN training across three architectural interventions: (i) UTS-Aug: augmenting with standard readout feature that encodes graph's true topology; (ii) UTS-Reg: topological regularizer that constrains representation collapse; (iii) UTS-Pool: topology-guided pooling that retains structurally critical nodes. We further leverage UTS as a layer-wise diagnostic to quantify oversmoothing during GNN training. Theoretically, we show that integrating UTS into GNN optimization strictly extends GNN expressivity beyond the 1-WL hierarchy. Experiments on three graph classification benchmarks show consistent benefits: Graph-UTS, Dual-UTS, and UTS-Pool improve accuracy across all three datasets, Embedding-UTS provides smaller but similarly consistent gains, and UTS-Reg's benefit varies across graph domains. Accuracy improves by up to 5.8% with Graph-UTS augmentation, by up to 1.9% with UTS-Reg, and achieves comparable performance to TOGL with UTS-Pool.

</details>


### [346] [High-Fidelity Digital Twin Data Models by Randomized Dynamic Mode Decomposition and Deep Learning with Applications in Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17101)
*Diana A. Bistrian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合随机化动态模态分解（randomized DMD）与深度学习人工智能的非侵入式框架，用于从数值代码输出中构建高保真数字孪生数据模型（DTM），并通过三个复杂度递增的冲击波算例验证了其数值精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降阶建模通常需要对控制方程进行 Galerkin 投影等侵入式操作，难以直接应用于复杂数值代码；作者希望以非侵入式方式从数值输出中识别高保真、低复杂度的数字孪生数据模型，使其能以高精度、低 CPU 时间与硬件成本重现原过程在时间上演化复杂的动力学行为。

Method: 作者首先将数字孪生数据模型（DTM）定义为一种复杂度降低、却能镜像原始过程行为的模型；随后提出新框架，将随机化动态模态分解（randomized DMD）与深度学习人工智能两种先进工具相结合，以非侵入式方式从数值代码输出中识别/构建高保真 DTM；并在复杂度递增的三个冲击波现象数值模拟中对该框架进行评估。

Result: 结果表明，所构建的数字孪生数据模型输出与原始源数据一致，同时具有复杂度降低的优势；在三个复杂度递增的冲击波数值算例中，新 DTM 在数值精度和计算效率两方面均得到全面评估并表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 结合随机化 DMD 与深度学习的新框架能够有效、非侵入式地构建高保真、低复杂度的数字孪生数据模型，在保持与原始数据一致的同时显著降低计算成本，适用于时间演化动力学复杂的难以探索场景。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is the identification of high-fidelity digital twin data models from numerical code outputs by non-intrusive techniques (i.e., not requiring Galerkin projection of the governing equations onto the reduced modes basis). In this paper the author defines the concept of the digital twin data model (DTM) as a model of reduced complexity that has the main feature of mirroring the original process behavior. The significant advantage of a DTM is to reproduce the dynamics with high accuracy and reduced costs in CPU time and hardware for settings difficult to explore because of the complexity of the dynamics over time. This paper introduces a new framework for creating efficient digital twin data models by combining two state-of-the-art tools: randomized dynamic mode decomposition and deep learning artificial intelligence. It is shown that the outputs are consistent with the original source data with the advantage of reduced complexity. The DTMs are investigated in the numerical simulation of three shock wave phenomena with increasing complexity. The author performs a thorough assessment of the performance of the new digital twin data models in terms of numerical accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [347] [Neural Field Ensembles for Aerodynamic Surface Prediction: Winning Solution to the ONERA CRM Wall Distribution 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17160)
*Lionel Salesses,Caroline Sainvitu,Tariq Benamara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件神经场的方法，在ONERA CRM壁面分布回归挑战赛中夺冠，用于预测NASA CRM复杂构型翼面压力与摩阻系数分布，在参数少三个量级的情况下超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD仿真计算成本高，机器学习代理模型是可行的替代方案；但针对真实飞机复杂几何、多流态以及训练数据有限的情况，构建精确代理模型仍很困难。为此需提出在有限数据下高效且稳健的气动代理建模方案。

Method: 将问题建模为条件神经场，把空间坐标、表面法向量和运行工况映射到气动壁面量（压力与摩阻系数）。逐步引入傅里叶特征编码、与竞赛指标对齐的相对平方误差目标、集成学习与k折交叉验证，通过消融研究记录各设计选择，并讨论多个被尝试后放弃的替代方案。

Result: 在隐藏竞赛测试集上取得总分8.81，优于最强组织方基线（8.64），且可训练参数量约少三个数量级，证明了坐标神经场在复杂几何有限数据下的高效与稳健。

Conclusion: 精心设计的坐标神经场是一种高效且稳健的气动代理建模框架，适用于复杂几何与有限数据条件，为替代高保真CFD提供可行路径。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogate models offer a promising alternative to high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations for aerodynamic analysis and design. However, constructing accurate surrogates for realistic aircraft configurations remain challenging due to complex geometries, multiple flow regimes, and limited training data. This work presents the methodology that achieved first place in the ONERA CRM Wall Distribution Regression Challenge, which focuses on predicting pressure and skin-friction coefficient distributions over the NASA Common Research Model wing-body-pylon-nacelle configuration under different operating conditions. The proposed approach formulates the problem as a conditional neural field mapping spatial coordinates, surface normals, and operating conditions to aerodynamic wall quantities. Fourier feature encoding, a relative squared error objective aligned with the challenge metric, ensemble learning, and $k$-fold cross-validation are progressively introduced to improve prediction accuracy and exploit the limited training data. Beyond presenting the final methodology, the paper documents the successive model design choices that led to the winning solution through a comprehensive ablation study and discusses several alternative approaches that were investigated but ultimately discarded. On the hidden competition test set, the proposed methodology achieves an overall score of 8.81, outperforming the strongest organizer-provided baseline, which achieved a score of 8.64, while requiring approximately three orders of magnitude fewer trainable parameters. These results illustrate that carefully designed coordinate-based neural fields constitute an efficient and robust framework for aerodynamic surrogate modeling on complex geometries under limited-data conditions.

</details>


### [348] [A unified framework for global and local interpretability using adaptive derivative-ordered random explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17171)
*Lemen Chao,Ming Lei,Anran Fanga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ADORE（Adaptive Derivative-Ordered Random Explanation），一个利用一阶与二阶导数、支持非线性特征交互与特征-样本交互的统一可解释性框架，并通过随机 SVD 与动态稀疏检测实现高效计算，在表格、文本、图像三大模态上优于 LIME、SHAP，同时以开源 Python 包发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂机器学习模型在高风险领域（如医疗、金融）中迫切需要可解释性，但现有事后（post-hoc）可解释方法存在四大缺陷：分析流程碎片化、对非线性特征交互建模能力不足、计算效率低、过度依赖特定模型架构。

Method: ADORE 采用一阶与二阶导数来刻画非线性模型复杂度，并在统一分析框架内捕获特征-样本交互；融合全局特征重要性与局部样本贡献，通过捕捉影响的大小和方向来精确定量特征影响，并识别影响模型决策的关键样本；借助随机化奇异值分解（SVD）和动态稀疏检测实现计算高效，从而可扩展到大规模高维数据集。

Result: 在表格、文本和图像三种数据模态上的实验表明，ADORE 在处理复杂交互和计算效率方面均优于 LIME、SHAP 等现有方法，并能提供详尽且可靠的解释。

Conclusion: ADORE 有效解决了既有事后可解释方法的局限，在复杂交互建模与计算效率上表现突出；为便于采用与复现，ADORE 已作为开源 Python 包在 GitHub 发布，方便研究者和实践者将其应用于各自的任务、模型和数据集。

Abstract: The interpretability of complex machine learning models is of paramount importance, especially in real-world high-stakes domains such as healthcare and finance. However, existing post-hoc interpretability methods suffer from inherent limitations: fragmented analytical processes, inadequate capacity to model nonlinear feature interactions, computational inefficiencies, and over-reliance on specific model architectures. To address these challenges, this paper provides a novel method - Adaptive Derivative-Ordered Random Explanation (ADORE) - that leverages first- and second-order derivatives to accommodate nonlinear model complexities, while enabling effective capture of feature-sample interactions within a unified analytical framework. ADORE integrates global feature importance with local sample contributions, precisely quantifying feature impact by capturing both magnitude and direction, and identifying critical samples influencing model decisions. Furthermore, it achieves computational efficiency through randomized singular value decomposition (SVD) and dynamic sparsity detection, making it scalable to large, high-dimensional datasets. Experiments across three data modalities - tabular, text, and image - demonstrate that ADORE outperforms existing methods such as LIME and SHAP in handling complex interactions and computational efficiency, while providing detailed and reliable explanations. To facilitate adoption and reproducibility, ADORE has been released as an open-source Python package, hosted on GitHub, enabling researchers and practitioners to readily adapt and apply our approach to their specific tasks, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [349] [IRENE: A Convolutional GRU Ensemble Model for Radar Precipitation Nowcasting over Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17175)
*Alessandro Camilletti,Gabriele Franch,Elena Tomasi,Marco Cristoforetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRENE 是一个面向意大利区域的深度学习概率降水短时临近预报模型，在 1km/5min 分辨率下优于 STEPS 和 DGMR 基准，其 GAN 及频谱约束变体在空间锐利度与小尺度方差方面表现不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率临近预报方法（如光流外推 STEPS 和预训练深度模型 DGMR）在概率预报技巧、集合校准或空间细节保持方面存在不足，需要一种能同时提升概率评分与集合校准、并在高时空分辨率（1km/5min）下工作的深度学习方法。

Method: 提出 IRENE，采用基于多尺度卷积门控循环单元（ConvGRU）的编码器-预报器架构，使用意大利民防部雷达合成数据训练；以重要性采样聚焦降水相关事件，以近似公平的连续排名概率分数（afCRPS）为主损失。另提出两个变体：IRENE-GAN 用对抗训练提升空间锐利度，IRENE-GAN-RAPSD 在对抗目标上增加径向平均功率谱密度（RAPSD）显式惩罚。

Result: 三个 IRENE 配置在所有预见期上 CRPS 均低于 STEPS 和 DGMR，排名直方图更接近均匀分布，表明概率技巧和集合校准更好；集合平均 MAE 的优势仅在前 90 分钟，之后强阻尼的 DGMR 和 STEPS 逐渐具有竞争力；谱分析显示对抗训练消除了 IRENE 小尺度方差的渐进损失，但代价是长预见期精细尺度能量过剩，频谱惩罚只能部分抑制。

Conclusion: IRENE 在概率评分和集合校准上全面优于基准，但确定性集合平均在中长预见期受阻尼问题影响；对抗训练改善了小尺度细节保持，却引入长预见期的小尺度能量过剩，频谱约束仅部分有效，提示概率预报质量与空间频谱保真之间的权衡仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: We present IRENE (Italian Radar Ensemble Nowcasting Experiment), a deep learning model for probabilistic short-range precipitation nowcasting over the Italian domain at \SI{1}{km} spatial and 5 min temporal resolution. IRENE adopts an encoder--forecaster architecture built on multi-scale Convolutional Gated Recurrent Units (ConvGRUs), trained on the national radar composite produced by the Italian Civil Protection Department (DPC). An importance-sampling scheme focuses training on precipitation-relevant events, while the almost-fair Continuous Ranked Probability Score (afCRPS) is adopted as the primary probabilistic loss function. Two additional training configurations are proposed: an adversarial (GAN) variant, IRENE-GAN, designed to improve the spatial sharpness of the generated forecasts, and a spectrally constrained variant, IRENE-GAN-RAPSD, in which the adversarial objective is complemented by an explicit penalty on the radially averaged power spectral density. The three configurations are evaluated against the stochastic extrapolation method STEPS and the pre-trained deep learning model DGMR. All IRENE configurations attain a lower Continuous Ranked Probability Score than both benchmarks at every lead time and rank histograms closer to uniformity, indicating better probabilistic skill and ensemble calibration. In terms of ensemble-mean mean absolute error the advantage is confined to the first 90 min, beyond which the strongly damped DGMR fields and, to a lesser extent, STEPS become competitive. Spectral analysis shows that the adversarial training removes the progressive loss of small-scale variance exhibited by IRENE, at the cost of an excess of fine-scale power at long lead times that the spectral penalty only partially controls.

</details>


### [350] [LoopSpec: Pipelined Self-Speculative Decoding for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17184)
*SangLyul Cho,Langqing Cui,Sehoon Kim,Dongsu Han,Insu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopSpec 是一种免训练的自投机解码框架，通过利用 Looped Transformer 的中间循环状态生成草稿 token 并以流水线方式重叠草稿生成与目标验证，结合深层二次提议，实现了高达 6.83 倍的推理加速且保持无损解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Transformer 因在每个循环深度重复访问共享权重，导致解码延迟高于同参数规模的标准 Transformer。为提升解码效率，自投机解码非常适合 Looped Transformer，因为其中间循环状态可直接提供草稿预测而无需辅助草稿模型。

Method: 提出 LoopSpec，一种针对 Looped Transformer 的免训练自投机解码框架。它从早期循环状态提取草稿 token，并以流水线方式运行，使未来 token 的草稿生成与当前 token 的目标验证重叠；引入来自更深循环深度的选择性二次提议以提升草稿精度，同时在贪心与采样两种模式下保证无损解码；此外推导出最优提议深度的闭式解，并验证预测与实测一致。

Result: 在推理与编程基准测试中，LoopSpec 在多种 Looped Transformer 上实现了高达 6.83 倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: LoopSpec 有效解决了 Looped Transformer 解码延迟高的问题，通过免训练的自投机解码实现了显著加速，且保持无损解码。

Abstract: Looped Transformers achieve strong performance with compact parameter sizes by repeatedly applying a shared stack of Transformer blocks across recurrent depths. However, they incur higher decoding latency than standard Transformer models of comparable parameter size because shared weights are accessed at every recurrent depth. To improve decoding efficiency, self-speculative decoding is particularly well suited to Looped Transformers, as their intermediate recurrent states can directly provide draft predictions without an auxiliary draft model. We therefore propose LoopSpec, a training-free self-speculative decoding framework tailored for Looped Transformers. LoopSpec extracts draft tokens from early recurrent states and operates in a pipelined manner, overlapping draft generation of future tokens with target verification of the current token. To improve draft accuracy without excessive compute overhead, we introduce a selective second proposal from deeper recurrent depth while ensuring lossless decoding under both greedy and sampling regimes. Furthermore, we derive the optimal proposal depths in closed form and show the prediction matches measurement. Across reasoning and coding benchmarks, LoopSpec achieves up to 6.83$\times$ inference speedup across diverse Looped Transformers.

</details>


### [351] [MyoFlow: Anchor-Tied Rectified Flow for HD-sEMG Gesture Recognition Across Sessions and Subjects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17194)
*Chenhao Wu,Dingjie Peng,Satoshi Funabashi,Satoshi Konishi,Wuqiang Yang,Hiroshi Onoda,Hironori Washizaki,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MyoFlow，首个面向 HD-sEMG 手势识别的判别式流匹配框架，将分类重构为“锚点绑定传输”，实现跨会话/跨受试者的零样本预测，并在两个数据集上显著超越最强扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HD-sEMG 手势识别受电极重新佩戴和生理变异影响，产生跨会话、跨受试者的分布偏移，降低识别精度；现有生成式模型主要用于数据增强，扩散模型虽能提升表征学习，但最终预测仍依赖独立分类器，未能将学到的动态与决策规则直接关联。

Method: 提出 MyoFlow，一个判别式流匹配框架：以域条件化的整流流（rectified flow）将编码后的时间窗向手势“锚点”传输，锚点同时作为传输目标和最近锚决策几何的中心，从而将分类直接构建在流匹配的传输过程上，无需独立分类头即可实现零样本预测。

Result: 在 Hyser 数据集上，MyoFlow 的平均跨会话与跨受试者准确率分别比最强扩散基线提高 4.24% 和 6.37%；在 CEMHSEY 数据集多天测试中，平均零样本准确率达 91.71%，平均少样本准确率达 97.39%。

Conclusion: 通过将学习到的动态与决策规则绑定，MyoFlow 有效缓解了 HD-sEMG 识别中的分布偏移问题，展现出优于现有扩散方法的跨会话、跨受试者泛化能力，并支持零样本与少样本场景，为鲁棒的肌电手势识别提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-density surface electromyography (HD-sEMG) gesture recognition supports prosthetic control, assistive robotics, and rehabilitation, but electrode re-donning and physiological variability cause distribution shifts that degrade accuracy across sessions and subjects. Generative HD-sEMG models primarily synthesize signals for augmentation; although diffusion models enhance representation learning, prediction still relies on a separate classifier. To tie learned dynamics to the decision rule, we propose MyoFlow, the first discriminative flow-matching framework for HD-sEMG recognition across sessions and subjects. It recasts classification as anchor-tied transport: a domain-conditioned rectified flow moves encoded windows toward gesture anchors that serve as transport targets and define the nearest-anchor decision geometry, enabling zero-shot prediction without an independent head. On the Hyser dataset, MyoFlow improves mean cross-session and cross-subject accuracy over the strongest diffusion-based baseline by 4.24\% and 6.37\%, respectively, and achieves 91.71\% mean zero-shot accuracy and 97.39\% mean few-shot accuracy across multiple days on the CEMHSEY dataset.

</details>


### [352] [Memorisation bias in medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17223)
*Moritz A. Knolle,Martin J. Menten,Laurin Lux,Mélanie Roschewitz,Emma A. M. Stanley,Georgios Kaissis,Daniel Rueckert,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种此前未被刻画的医疗AI风险——"记忆偏置"（memorisation bias）：当模型在训练期间见过某患者的匿名历史数据后，对该患者未来新数据的预测会发生显著改变，并可能持续数十年。在模拟前瞻部署中，这种偏置对返回复诊的数据贡献者产生不对称影响：患者出现历史记录中没有的新病症时诊断敏感性显著下降，而健康状态未变时敏感性和特异性则被显著高估。作者指出，现行去标识化措施使临床难以识别并排除返回的贡献者，因此亟需改进模型训练与部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI模型常会无意中记住训练集中个体的记录，此前研究多关注这种记忆带来的定向隐私攻击，但其对临床实际部署的影响（即患者可能被一个在训练期间见过其历史数据的模型所评估）尚不清楚。作者旨在探究这一被忽视的临床后果。

Method: 作者定义并系统验证了"记忆偏置"现象，在多种数据模态（diverse data modalities）和模型架构（model architectures）上开展实验，检验模型是否因见过患者的历史匿名数据而改变对其未来数据的预测；并进一步设计模拟前瞻部署（simulated prospective deployment），比较训练过与未训练过该患者历史数据的模型，在患者复诊时的诊断敏感性（sensitivity）与特异性（specificity）变化。

Result: 1) 记忆偏置在多种数据模态与模型架构中普遍存在，且在部分情况下可延续至数十年后的未来记录上；2) 模拟前瞻部署中，当患者以历史记录中不存在的新病症（de novo condition）复诊时，训练过其历史数据的模型诊断敏感性显著下降；3) 当患者健康状态未变时，其敏感性和特异性均被显著高估，呈不对称影响。

Conclusion: 这项研究揭示了医疗AI中一个此前未被刻画的风险：当模型部署在曾为其训练数据做出贡献的患者身上时会产生记忆偏置。现行去标识化措施虽旨在保护隐私，却使识别返回的数据贡献者并将其排除在模型对自身未来数据的辅助解读之外变得困难。因此，缓解记忆风险可能需要改变现有的模型训练与部署规范。

Abstract: Medical AI models hold immense potential to improve patient outcomes, but they are also known to unintentionally memorise individual records from their training datasets. While such memorisation has been linked to targeted privacy attacks, its consequences for clinical deployment, where patients may be assessed by a model that saw their historical data during training, remain poorly understood. Here we show that predictions on a patient's unseen future data can change significantly if a model observed that same patient's anonymised historical data during training, a phenomenon we term "memorisation bias". We demonstrate that this bias exists across diverse data modalities and model architectures, and over prolonged time spans: in some cases, memorisation bias persists on future records acquired decades after the historical records used for training. Moreover, in simulated prospective deployment, memorisation bias has asymmetric effects on the diagnostic accuracy of returning data contributors. When a patient returned with a de novo condition absent from their historical records in the training dataset, diagnostic sensitivity decreased significantly compared to an otherwise identical model not trained on their historical data. Conversely, when their health state was unchanged, both sensitivity and specificity were significantly inflated. Our findings reveal a previously uncharacterised risk in medical AI that arises when a model is deployed on patients who contributed to its training data. This exposes a shortcoming of current model development practice: the de-identification measures designed to protect patients' privacy make it difficult to identify returning contributors and exclude them from the AI-assisted interpretation of their own future data. Mitigating memorisation risks may thus require changes to current model training and deployment protocols.

</details>


### [353] [Easy to Catch a Liar, Hard to Clear an Honest One: Language Models Diagnosing a Corrupted Reward Channel from a Verified Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17226)
*Arman Nik Khah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结的大语言模型在获得关于"报告者"的验证性数据后，能否区分诚实与撒谎的报告者。结果发现模型几乎完美识破撒谎者，却严重误判诚实者为撒谎者，且误判率受无关表面特征影响，表现差于预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中智能体只能依赖奖励报告，当奖励突然改变时，无法从报告本身区分"世界变了"与"报告者坏了"；理论上需要关于报告者本身的额外数据才能摆脱困境。作者想检验冻结的语言模型在获得这类验证性数据后，是否真的会利用它来判断报告者的诚实性。

Method: 构建一个两选项博弈：设计"支付交换"与"撒谎报告者"两种情形，使其产生的历史记录逐字节相同。然后加入一条已验证记录（对某一轮真实结果的独立核查，与报告者对该轮的说法并列呈现）。要求三个大模型（两个家族，规模为70B和32B）仅用一个字母回答"报告者是诚实还是在撒谎"。通过在轮次、字母、措辞等维度上变换提示，系统测量模型的判断准确率及相关表面特征的影响。

Result: 模型几乎完美识别撒谎的报告者：70B类模型在所有尝试条件下均成立，32B模型仅在一种措辞下失误。但模型对诚实报告者的判定差得多：72B模型在"什么都没变"时38%误判为撒谎者、"支付变动"时58%误判；另一家族的70B模型分别为26%和48%。模型并非读不懂——当答案直接印在提示中时，同一批模型得分0.96至1.00。驱动误判的表面特征因家族而异：Qwen系看"记录指向哪一轮"，Llama系看"哪个字母代表诚实"。甚至把该记录加入已陈述答案的提示，反而让Llama更不可能给出该答案。作者事先注册的预测值为35%，实际58%的误判大于预期。

Conclusion: 冻结的大语言模型虽有验证性数据，仍未能可靠地区分诚实与撒谎的报告者，主要问题不是识破谎言，而是对诚实者的大量误判，且误判受与任务无关的表面特征操控，表现比预期更差。说明模型并未像理论所期望的那样有效利用关于报告者本身的数据。

Abstract: An agent that learns from rewards has to trust whatever reports those rewards. When the reports suddenly change, either the world changed or the reporter broke. From the reports alone these are indistinguishable, and reinforcement learning theory shows that no amount of further experience separates them. The prescribed escape is richer data about the reporter itself. We ask whether a frozen language model, handed exactly that data, uses it. We build a two-option game in which a payout swap and a lying reporter produce byte-identical histories. Then we add one verified record: an independent check of one round's real result, printed beside what the reporter said about that round. That single line settles the case. We ask three large models, from two families, to answer one question with one letter. Is the reporter honest or lying? They catch a lying reporter almost perfectly. At the 70B class that holds in every condition we tried; the 32B model slips in one wording. They clear an honest reporter far less often, and how often depends on things that should not matter. Averaged over rounds, letters, and wordings, a 72B model calls an honest reporter a liar 38% of the time when nothing has changed at all, and 58% of the time when the payouts moved. A 70B model from a second family calls an honest reporter a liar 26% and 48% of the time. The failure is not one of reading, because in the situation where nothing changed the same models score 0.96 to 1.00 with the answer printed in the prompt. Which surface feature drives it differs by family. For the Qwen models it is which round the record names, and for Llama it is which letter stands for "honest." Adding the record to a prompt that already states the answer makes Llama less likely to give that answer. We had registered a prediction for that 58% before the run: 35%. The failure is larger than we expected.

</details>


### [354] [Same Flow, Different Paths: Variance Reduction in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17287)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从随机梯度方差的角度研究流匹配（Flow Matching）中路径 g_t 的选择问题，发现即使FM目标函数完全相同，不同路径也能从根本上改变SGD的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配中路径 g_t 的选择通常被视为超参数，其理论影响尚不清晰。作者希望通过优化视角研究路径选择，揭示即使诱导相同的边缘分布与速度场（即相同FM目标），路径也会通过随机梯度方差显著影响SGD收敛速率。

Method: 作者分析定义一类路径 G(p_t, v_t*)——它们诱导相同的边缘分布 p_t 与边缘速度场 v_t*，因而对应相同的FM目标。(i) 对线性速度模型与一维高斯数据，推导出SGD迭代复杂度（精确到对数因子）的紧界，并在线性路径族中解析求出最小化该界的路径。(ii) 将方差分析推广到一般FM问题，在固定参数 θ 下将路径选择形式化为方差最小化问题 PathOpt_θ，并限制 g_t ∈ G(p_t, v_t*)。(iii) 由于该约束通常无法直接验证，作者推导出一个可由样本估计约束的等价形式，从而能够数值求解路径。并用高斯数据、高斯混合模型及真实数据集进行实验验证。

Result: 主要结论是路径 g_t 的选择会从根本上改变SGD的收敛速率，即使FM目标完全相同。作者证明约束 g_t ∈ G(p_t, v_t*) 是必要的——忽略该约束仅降低方差反而可能导致收敛更慢；并提出可数值求解的等价约束形式。

Conclusion: 路径选择对FM的SGD收敛速率具有根本性影响，应当视为优化问题的一部分而非随意设定的超参数。通过在维持相同FM问题（约束在 G(p_t, v_t*) 内）的前提下最小化随机梯度方差，可以显著加速收敛，理论和实验均验证了这一点。

Abstract: In flow matching (FM), a velocity model $v_θ$ is trained using a predefined path $g_t$ that connects data and noise samples (e.g., $g_t(x_0, x_1) = (1 - t) x_0 + t x_1$). In this work, we study the choice of this path from an optimization perspective by analyzing the variance of stochastic gradients. We consider the class $G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ of paths that induce the same marginal distributions $p_t$ and marginal velocity field $v^\star_t$, and therefore the same FM objective. Our main finding is that the choice of path $g_t$ can fundamentally change the convergence rate of SGD, even when the FM objective remains exactly the same. (i) For a linear velocity model and one-dimensional Gaussian data, we derive a tight bound on the SGD iteration complexity up to logarithmic factors and find an analytically optimal path that minimizes this bound among linear paths inducing the same FM problem. (ii) We then extend the variance analysis to general FM problems and formulate path selection at a fixed $θ$ as the variance-minimization problem PathOpt$_θ$, constrained to $g_t\in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$. We show that this constraint is essential: reducing variance without it can lead to slower convergence. (iii) Since the constraint $g_t \in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ cannot generally be verified directly, we derive an equivalent formulation with constraints that can be estimated from samples, allowing paths to be found numerically. Our theoretical results are supported by experiments with Gaussian data, Gaussian mixture models, and real datasets.

</details>


### [355] [Large Language Models Develop Belief State Geometry In-Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17376)
*Daniel Balcells,Andrew Jun Lee,Chirag Rastogi,Paul M. Riechers,Adam Shai,Xavier Poncini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控的隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）提示设置下，研究大语言模型（LLMs）中支撑上下文学习（ICL）的表征，发现信念状态可从残差流激活中线性解码，且对该子空间的干预能维持或影响下游预测，表明 LLM 的 ICL 近似于对上下文推断生成模型的最优贝叶斯预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs 基于下一 token 预测训练，展现出强大的上下文学习能力，但支撑 ICL 的表征机制仍不清晰。作者希望通过受控实验（用 HMM 产生的数据提示 LLM）来定位并理解与"信念状态"（给定观测历史时对隐状态的后验分布）相关的内部表征，从而在表征层面解释 ICL。

Method: 作者选取 6 个开源 LLM，用 40 个经过筛选、具有非平凡信念结构的 HMM 生成的数据进行提示；通过线性探针（probing）检测残差流激活中是否可线性解码出对应的信念状态；随后在探针识别出的子空间上直接进行 patching 和 steering 干预，并设置消融对照，以验证该表征的功能相关性。

Result: 信念状态可从残差流激活中被线性解码，在所有 HMM 与 LLM 组合中，探针的峰值 R² 值在 0.83–0.99 之间，且出现在从早期到后期的不同层；对探针识别子空间的直接干预使下游预测质量接近未经干扰的模型，而对照干预则显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 研究提供了表征层面的证据，证明开源 LLM 的上下文学习近似于对上下文推断的生成模型进行最优贝叶斯预测；并将"输入分布结构与激活几何相关"的结论从显式训练于 HMM 数据的玩具网络拓展到生产规模的 LLM。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on next-token prediction exhibit remarkable in-context learning (ICL) abilities, yet the representations that support ICL remain poorly understood. We consider such representations in a controlled setting: prompting LLMs with data emitted from hidden Markov models (HMMs) and probing for the corresponding belief state -- the posterior distribution over the HMM's hidden states given the observed token history. Across six open-source LLMs prompted with data from 40 HMMs selected for non-trivial belief structure, we find that belief states are linearly decodable from residual stream activations, with peak probe $R^2$-values from 0.83-0.99 across HMM and LLM combinations, ranging from early to late layers. To establish functional relevance, we intervene directly on the probe-identified subspace via patching and steering, resulting in downstream prediction quality on the order of the untampered model, while controls degrade performance substantially. Together, these results provide representation-level evidence that ICL in open-source LLMs approximates optimal Bayesian prediction over a context-inferred generative model. More broadly, our findings extend prior results linking input-distribution structure to activation geometry: from toy networks trained explicitly on HMM data to production-scale LLMs.

</details>


### [356] [OPEN-1B: A Fully Auditable Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17380)
*John Donaghy,Brian Wilcox,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Shikhar Rastogi,Adam St Arnaud,Alexey Titov,Jordan Greenberg,Ben Fielding,Harry Grieve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"完全可审计"的模型透明度新层级，通过消除浮点非结合性、GPU核归约、数据批序及集合通信等训练不确定性来源，使大规模分布式训练的每一步都能在异构商品硬件上以位级确定性复现，并配套集体验证方案与开源模型 Open-1B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源语言模型存在可复现性问题：尽管公开了权重、训练数据和配方，但因浮点运算的非结合性，无法证明发布的检查点确实由声明的训练配方产生，留下了未公开数据、注入偏见或后门的空间，而现有的 proof-of-learning 或 proof-of-training-data 技术无法排除这些风险。

Method: 作者引入"完全可审计"这一新的模型透明度层级，通过为三类训练不确定性来源（GPU核归约、数据并行集群中的数据批排序、节点间/节点内集合通信）强制设定确定顺序，使大规模分布式训练的任意单个步骤都能在单一商品硬件上按位精确重放。由于在单机上重放整个训练不可行，进一步设计了集体验证方案，由多个独立审计者各自认证不同步骤，共同覆盖整次运行。

Result: 发布了 Open-1B 模型及其完整预训练数据集、所有中间检查点、训练代码库和审计工具链（audit harness），使任何人都能复现并验证其训练的任意步骤。

Conclusion: 通过确定化训练执行与集体验证机制，可实现模型的真正可复现与可审计，显著提升开源语言模型的透明度与可信度，有效消除对未公开数据、偏见注入或后门的担忧。

Abstract: Open-source language models have a reproducibility problem. Despite releasing weights, training data, and recipes, none of them are provably reproducible due to the non-associativity of floating-point arithmetic. Deep learning frameworks often offer a deterministic execution mode, allowing reproducible operations on the same machines. Unfortunately, this determinism does not carry across hardware such that a user can verify that a released checkpoint was actually produced using the declared training recipe. This leaves room for undisclosed data, injected biases, or backdoors that existing techniques such as proof-of-learning or proof-of-training-data cannot rule out.
  We introduce a new tier of model transparency, fully auditable, in which every operation on every data sample during training is independently reproducible on heterogeneous commodity hardware with bitwise certainty. By imposing a definite order on the sources of training nondeterminism, GPU kernel reductions, data batch ordering across a data-parallel cluster, and inter/intra-node collective communication, we make it possible to replay any individual step of a large, distributed training run on a single piece of commodity hardware and check it against the published trajectory.
  Because replaying an entire run on one machine is infeasible, we support this with a collective verification scheme in which many independent auditors each certify individual steps, together covering the whole run. We release Open-1B, a model trained under this regime, together with its full pretraining dataset, every intermediate checkpoint, the training codebase, and the audit harness needed to reproduce and verify any step of its training.

</details>


### [357] [Bridging the Confidence Gap: Temperature Scaling for Calibrating Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17386)
*Yuwei Liang,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Yinuo Xu,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoTS 与 E-CoTS 两种事后校准方法，通过温度缩放缩小自适应预测与零样本预测之间的置信度差距，并结合弱-强集成策略，在不损失甚至提升准确率的前提下显著降低测试时提示调优（TPT）的校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时提示调优（TPT）虽能提升单实例自适应准确率，但往往牺牲校准性能；而现有校准方法通常引入额外正则项来增强文本嵌入分散度、降低校准误差，却常常导致准确率下降。受零样本预测本身校准良好的启发，作者希望设计一种在保持准确率的同时实现良好校准的事后校准方法。

Method: 提出 CoTS：对（自适应后的）预测施加温度缩放，以最小化自适应预测与零样本预测之间的置信度差距，实现事后校准且不损害准确率。进一步利用自适应过程中的多种数据增强，引入弱-强集成策略（weak-strong ensemble）以进一步提升准确率，并对该集成结果应用 CoTS，得到 E-CoTS，从而保持集成模型的良好校准特性。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种骨干网络上的实验表明，所提方法能有效缓解失校准问题且不牺牲主要准确率。例如，在 ImageNet 变体上，E-CoTS 将 TPT 的平均期望校准误差（ECE）从 11.90% 降至 5.38%，同时将准确率从 60.74% 提升到 62.95%。此外，与现有校准方法结合时，E-CoTS 通常能同时提升准确率与校准性能。

Conclusion: CoTS 与 E-CoTS 是简单且有效的事后校准方法，能够在保持或提升准确率的同时显著缓解 TPT 的失校准问题，并可与其他现有校准方法协同工作，同时改善准确率与校准表现。

Abstract: Test-time prompt tuning (TPT) enables adaptation on a single test instance, achieving improved accuracy but often sacrificing calibration performance. Most existing calibration methods introduce additional regularization terms to promote dispersion across text embeddings and reduce calibration error, yet these methods often suffer from a drop in accuracy. Motivated by the well-calibrated nature of zero-shot predictions, we propose CoTS, a simple yet effective post-hoc calibration method that preserves accuracy. Specifically, CoTS applies temperature scaling to minimize the confidence gap between adapted and zero-shot predictions. To fully exploit the potential of multiple augmentations during adaptation, we introduce a weak-strong ensemble strategy that further boosts accuracy. We then apply CoTS to this ensemble, termed E-CoTS, to maintain its well-calibrated property. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets and backbones show that our approaches effectively mitigate miscalibration without compromising primary accuracy. For instance, E-CoTS reduces the average expected calibration error of TPT from 11.90% to 5.38% on ImageNet variants, while even increasing accuracy from 60.74% to 62.95%. Moreover, when integrated with existing calibration methods, E-CoTS usually enhances both accuracy and calibration simultaneously.

</details>


### [358] [Knowledge as Orbit: Finite Collections as Phases of an Exactly Periodic Latent Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17417)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将有限集合知识以"内涵方式"存储：用学习到的潜在空间中的固定旋转轨道（实离散傅里叶算子，保证 R^X = I 精确闭环）作为码，配合共享解码器，实现图像的紧凑循环表示与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限知识存储是外延式的（每个对象一个码/向量），作者追问能否改为内涵式存储——用一条精确回到起点的紧凑"定律"的轨道来解码整个有限集合，从而在循环视频等场景获得精确闭环、压缩与生成能力。

Method: 将第 i 个对象编码为学习潜在空间中固定旋转的第 i 相位，所有相位由共享网络解码；潜在变量通过一组整数谐波旋转（实离散傅里叶算子）推进，构成一个满足 R^X = I 的精确周期算子。通过对比实验：固定解码器、仅改变算子的情况下，比较一般学习算子、保范但非周期算子、以及精确周期算子的表现；还对比密集解码器与卷积解码器的容量/压缩权衡，并以码本为对照验证生成定律在重建代价上的"免费性"。

Result: 在固定解码器下，一般学习算子发散，保范非周期算子在环路附近退化，而精确周期算子保持平坦，且在真实图像上差距更大。密集解码器对每张清晰图像存在与尺寸无关的结构性开销，无法与压缩兼容；小型卷积解码器在共享流形的对象上能达到既清晰又紧凑。码本对照显示生成定律在重建意义上免费，却使潜在存储成倍增加。在七个基准视频片段上，与匹配的帧索引基线相比，循环模型在相同参数下达到同等或更好的保真度，并以机器精度闭环，而基线留有可见接缝；将基线频率固定到循环谐波后其接缝也闭合，证明精确周期性是关键约束。

Conclusion: 有限循环知识可以作为动力学而非独立实例来存储，精确递归由代数提供，内容由共享解码器提供，从而同时实现精确闭环、紧凑表示与高质量生成。

Abstract: Finite knowledge is usually stored extensionally, one code or vector per item. We ask whether a finite collection can instead be stored intensionally, as the decoded orbit of one compact law that returns exactly to its start. For X objects, we encode item i as the i-th phase of a fixed rotation in a learned latent space and decode all phases with a shared network; the latent advances through a bank of rotations at integer harmonics of the cycle, a real discrete Fourier operator, so that R^X equals the identity and exact closure is guaranteed rather than learned. Images are a controlled carrier; looping video is the case where the phase order is the content's own temporal structure. Holding the decoder fixed and varying only the operator, a general learned operator diverges, a norm-preserving but non-periodic one degrades around the loop, and the exactly periodic operator is flat; on real images the gap widens. Capacity is then the decoder's budget: dense decoders carry a structural overhead per crisp image that no size reconciles with compression, while a small convolutional decoder on objects that share a manifold reaches crisp and compressed. A codebook control shows the generative law is free in reconstruction terms while multiplying the latent store many-fold. On seven benchmark clips, against a matched frame-index baseline, the cycle reaches equal or better fidelity at equal parameters while wrapping at machine precision, where the baseline leaves a visible seam; pinning the baseline's frequencies to loop harmonics closes its seam too, confirming that exact periodicity is the operative constraint. Finite cyclic knowledge can be stored as dynamics rather than independent instances, with exact recurrence supplied by algebra and content by a shared decoder.

</details>


### [359] [Learning-Guided Planning in Large Dynamic Action Spaces: Budgeted Tree Search for One-to-Many Mobile Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17429)
*Liang-Ching Tao,Pi-Chung Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LP-BTS，一种学习引导的规划架构，用于解决大规模、动态、几何约束的动作空间下的序列决策问题（以一对多移动充电为具体场景）。它用图提议策略压缩候选动作支持、用学习价值评判器评估叶子节点、并用带边预算的 PUCT 比较短程模拟未来后再决策。单个冻结模型即可覆盖 736 至 2,813 个动作的多种设定，并在密封的 30 场景确认集上取得最高存活率与 alive-AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习型序列决策系统直接由当前状态映射到动作，当候选动作数量庞大、具有几何结构且随状态重建时，这种直接映射变得脆弱。一对多移动充电是一个典型实例：250 个传感器初始状态下产生约 1,125 个候选充电停靠点动作，每次选中动作同时服务其覆盖范围内的传感器，且动作空间会随传感器死亡而动态变化。需要在这样的动态大动作空间中做出稳健决策。

Method: LP-BTS 采用学习引导的规划架构，包含三个组件：(1) 图提议策略（graph proposal policy）将候选动作浓缩到一个小型支持集；(2) 学习型价值评判器（value critic）评估叶子节点；(3) 带边预算的 PUCT（edge-budgeted PUCT）在做出最终动作前比较若干短程模拟未来。由于策略在无需固定输出头的情况下对该集合打分，单个冻结检查点即可覆盖从 736 到 2,813 个动作的所有评估设定。实验采用匹配消融：均匀采样、固定目标支持下的 PUCT、直接策略选择等对照。

Result: 匹配消融显示组件互补：均匀采样损失 8.8 个存活百分点；在固定目标支持条件下，PUCT 联合保留 1.4 个百分点（约 250 个传感器中的 3.5 个），而直接策略选择的行驶距离增加 23%。在预先指定、密封的 30 场景确认集上一次评估中，LP-BTS 取得最高存活率（0.4545）和 alive-AUC（0.8031）。相对于最强领域工程化对照方法的估计存活优势为 +0.0066（95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]），差异未达显著；但在每个配对场景上均超过一个截止期启发式和两个源派生的直接策略重构。两个学习行均是从 Gong 等人报告变体的训练、源派生重构。

Conclusion: 在大规模动态动作空间场景中，该结果提供了关于学习引导规划的可控证据。LP-BTS 通过策略提议、价值评判与 PUCT 规划的互补结合，在动作空间随状态变化且规模庞大时表现出优于直接策略映射和领域工程化方法的趋势，尽管相对最强工程化对照的存活优势在统计上仍未解决。

Abstract: Many learned sequential decision systems map the current state directly to an action. That shortcut becomes brittle when candidate actions are numerous, geometrically structured, and rebuilt with the state. One-to-many mobile charging makes this setting concrete: with N=250 sensors, the initial state induces about 1,125 candidate charging-stop actions; each chosen stop simultaneously serves its in-range sensors, and the action universe changes as sensors die. LP-BTS is a learning-guided planning architecture: a graph proposal policy concentrates a small candidate support, a learned value critic evaluates leaves, and edge-budgeted PUCT compares short simulated futures before committing an action. Because the policy scores this set without a fixed output head, a single frozen checkpoint covers every evaluated setting, spanning action universes from 736 to 2,813 stops. Matched ablations reveal complementary effects: uniform sampling costs 8.8 survival percentage points, while, with targeted support fixed, PUCT jointly retains 1.4 points (about 3.5 of 250 sensors) and direct policy selection travels 23% farther. On a prospectively specified, sealed 30-scenario confirmatory bank evaluated once, LP-BTS attains the highest observed survival (0.4545) and alive-AUC (0.8031). Its estimated survival advantage over the strongest domain-engineered comparator is +0.0066 (95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]), an unresolved difference, while it exceeds a deadline heuristic and two source-derived direct-policy reconstructions on every paired scenario. Both learned rows are trained, source-derived reconstructions of variants reported by Gong et al. In this setting, the results provide controlled evidence about learning-guided planning in a large, dynamic action space.

</details>


### [360] [Reduced-Space Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization of Process Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17440)
*Niki Triantafyllou,Andrea Bernardi,Maria M. Papathanasiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降维空间多保真贝叶斯优化框架（RS-MFBO），用于解决工业流程设计中高维、昂贵黑箱函数的优化难题，通过全局敏感性分析降维、保真度增强的高斯过程以及带冷却与晋升机制的成本感知采集策略，在两个工业流程模拟器上验证其显著减少高保真评估次数并保持竞争性的优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业流程 flowsheet 的优化常常在计算上不可行，原因在于严格（rigorous）模拟的高昂成本，以及复杂设计空间固有的“维度灾难”。因此需要一种能同时应对高维度和高评估成本的黑箱优化方法。

Method: 提出 RS-MFBO 框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 利用全局敏感性分析（GSA）进行降维；(2) 采用保真度增强（fidelity-augmented）的高斯过程，捕捉低成本近似与昂贵高保真评估之间的相关性；(3) 设计成本感知的采集（acquisition）策略，并加入冷却（cooldown）与晋升（promotion）机制，自适应地引导样本在不同保真度层级之间的分配。

Result: 在两个工业流程模拟器（SuperPro Designer 中的质粒 DNA 生物过程、Aspen HYSYS 中的绿色燃料合成装置）上验证，针对多种经济与物理目标的结果表明：与单保真度基线相比，该方法大幅减少了高保真模拟器的评估次数，同时保持了有竞争力的优化性能。

Conclusion: RS-MFBO 是一种可扩展、与具体模拟器无关（simulator-agnostic）的方法，适用于成本受限的黑箱优化场景，能有效缓解工业流程优化中的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Optimizing industrial process flowsheets is often computationally prohibitive due to the high cost of rigorous simulations and the curse of dimensionality inherent in complex design spaces. To address these challenges, we present a reduced-space multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (RS-MFBO) framework designed for high-dimensional, expensive black-box functions. The approach integrates Global Sensitivity Analysis (GSA) for dimensionality reduction with a fidelity-augmented Gaussian process that captures correlations between low-cost approximations and expensive high-fidelity evaluations. A cost-aware acquisition strategy, augmented with cooldown and promotion mechanisms, adaptively guides the allocation of samples across fidelities. The framework is validated on two distinct industrial process simulators: a plasmid DNA bioprocess in SuperPro Designer and a green fuel synthesis plant in Aspen HYSYS. Results across diverse economic and physical objectives demonstrate that the proposed method substantially reduces the number of high-fidelity simulator evaluations while maintaining competitive optimization performance compared to single-fidelity baselines. These results highlight RS-MFBO as a scalable, simulator-agnostic approach for cost-constrained black-box optimization.

</details>


### [361] [FreqSpaNet: Frequency and Spatial Learning of SFPF for Physical Layer Hardware Integrity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17491)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,YiZhe Wang,Meng Zhang,Xian Li,Yuying Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FreqSpaNet，一种用于开放集硬件异常检测的空频极化指纹表示学习网络，通过频率分支与几何感知空间分支分别建模不同结构依赖，并以自适应融合与互补预训练提升识别性能，AUROC 达 96.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经授权的硬件替换可在保留无线设备逻辑身份的同时改变其物理实现，对硬件完整性验证构成挑战。空频极化指纹（SFPFs）能捕捉设备在多个频率和方向上的依赖响应，但其频率维度与空间维度呈现不同的结构依赖关系，现有方法难以同时有效建模这两类异质性特征。

Method: 提出 FreqSpaNet 网络：频率分支捕捉相邻频率间的局部变化，几何感知空间分支利用角度信息对方向关系建模；两个分支的表征经自适应融合组合，并采用互补预训练在保留频率与空间表征各自特性的同时捕捉共享信息。

Result: 实验表明 FreqSpaNet 平均 AUROC 达到 96.31%，较基线高出 9.05 个百分点；在七种硬件替换场景下的结果进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: FreqSpaNet 通过分别建模 SFPF 的频率与空间结构依赖并加以融合，有效提升了开放集硬件异常检测性能，能够应对硬件替换带来的物理实现与逻辑身份不一致问题。

Abstract: Unauthorized hardware replacement can preserve a wireless device's logical identity while altering its physical implementation, posing a challenge to hardware integrity verification. Spatio-frequency polarization fingerprints (SFPFs) capture device-dependent responses across multiple frequencies and directions, but their frequency and spatial dimensions exhibit different structural dependencies. We propose FreqSpaNet, an SFPF representation learning network for open set hardware anomaly detection. A frequency branch captures local variations among neighboring frequencies, while a geometry-aware spatial branch models directional relationships using angular information. The two representations are combined through adaptive fusion, and complementary pretraining further captures shared information while preserving the distinct characteristics of the frequency and spatial representations. Experiments show that FreqSpaNet achieves a mean AUROC of 96.31\%, 9.05 points above the baseline. Results under seven hardware replacement scenarios further verify the effectiveness of FreqSpaNet.

</details>


### [362] [ENCP: Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17499)
*Vicky Feliren,A. Taufiq Asyhari,Muhamad Risqi U. Saputra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ENCP（Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction），一种针对视觉语言导航（VLN）模型的不确定性估计方法。它通过按策略残差置信度重新缩放非一致性分数，并在每个 episode 上校准一个最大分数，实现了在步骤间存在依赖关系的可变长度导航任务上的有效覆盖率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 模型的不确定性估计对于识别模糊和不可靠的预测至关重要，可帮助智能体做出更安全的导航决策。共形预测（CP）是最先进的不确定性估计框架之一，但 VLN 智能体需要一系列步骤，标准校准无法在依赖的、可变长度的 episode 上提供其承诺的覆盖率保证。因此需要一种能适应 VLN 序列结构特点的共形预测方法。

Method: 提出 Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction（ENCP）。核心思想是：用策略的残差置信度（residual confidence）对非一致性分数（nonconformity score）进行重新缩放，并每个 episode 只校准一个最大分数。在可交换的校准与测试 episode 假设下，该构造保证每一步都以至少 1 - α 的概率覆盖真实值，同时允许 episode 内部步骤之间存在依赖性。

Result: 在 R2R 和 REVERIE 数据集上，对四种 VLN 策略和三种非一致性分数进行验证，ENCP 在 seen-to-unseen 评估中达到了所有报告的实证逐步覆盖率（step-coverage）目标。

Conclusion: ENCP 提供了与模型无关的不确定性估计，可用于判断 VLN 智能体何时应让渡给能力更强的预测器（包括人工协助），从而增强导航决策的安全性。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation for Vision-Language-Navigation (VLN) models is a critical task since it can help identify ambiguous and unreliable predictions, enabling agents to make safer navigation decisions. As one of the most advanced uncertainty estimation frameworks, conformal prediction (CP) offers a promising approach for uncertainty estimation in VLN. However, given that VLN agent requires a sequence of steps, standard calibration in conformal prediction fails to provide coverage guarantee it promises over a dependent, variable-length VLN episode. To this end, we propose Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP), which rescales a nonconformity score by the policy's residual confidence and calibrates one maximum score per episode. Under exchangeable calibration and test episodes, this construction covers the ground truth at every step with probability at least $1 - α$, while allowing dependence among steps within an episode. Across four VLN policies and three nonconformity scores on R2R and REVERIE dataset, ENCP meets all reported empirical step-coverage targets on the seen-to-unseen evaluation. These results demonstrate that ENCP can provide model-agnostic uncertainty estimates, which might be useful for determining when a VLN agent should defer to a more capable predictor, including human assistance.

</details>
